We prove the first minimax lower bounds of $Ω(K\log(T/K))$ for QMAB and $Ω(d\log(T/d))$ for finite-action QLB, resolving the question raised by Wan et al. [2023] of whether regret independent of $T$ is achievable. At the heart of our argument is a high-confidence single-arm quantum testing lower bound for distinguishing a fixed reward mean from an interval of alternatives, proved by the polynomial method and a Remez-type inequality for trigonometric polynomials. A bandit-to-testing reduction then lifts it to the QMAB lower bound, while a linear embedding gives the finite-action QLB lower bound. Complementing the lower bounds, we give a design-based elimination algorithm for finite-action QLB. When the action set has size $\operatorname{poly}(d)$, its regret is linear in $d$, improving the prior $d^2$ dependence and matching our lower bound up to polylogarithmic factors. The algorithm couples a low-bias low-variance quantum mean estimator with a small-support $G$-optimal design through a query allocation matched to the design weights. The design-based elimination reduces the dimension dependence from $d^2$ to $d^{3/2}$ when using Quantum Monte Carlo estimates. The low-variance estimator then makes reconstruction error aggregate through variance rather than worst-case absolute error, removing the remaining $\sqrt d$ factor.

</details>


### [157] [Non-Parametric Spatiotemporal Trajectory Prediction via State-Conditioned Transition Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14349)
*Michael Fore,Akshay Jain,Justin Downes,Rohan Pradhan,Duncan Botti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种免训练的多模态轨迹预测方法，无需GPU和任何可学习参数，即可达到与5700万参数Transformer相当的精度，且在数据稀缺场景下显著优于Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的轨迹预测方法（如Transformer）需要大量训练数据和GPU资源，在数据稀缺的新地理区域部署困难，因此需要一种无需训练、数据需求低、计算资源少但精度可比的替代方案。

Method: 构建历史状态到下一位置的转移表，使用基于空间邻近性、方位角、速度和时序上下文的乘积核检索邻居；在此共享表示上支持两种推理模式：多样性惩罚采样（生成覆盖不同可行路线的轨迹）和束搜索（寻找最高似然路径）。

Result: 在TrAISformer基准（丹麦海事AIS数据）上，全量数据下精度与Transformer相当；数据稀缺时优势显著——在仅10%训练数据下仍保持稳定，而TrAISformer性能灾难性下降。

Conclusion: 该方法可用少一个数量级的历史数据部署到新地理区域，且无需GPU训练，兼具部署成本低与数据效率高的优势。

Abstract: We present a training-free method for multi-modal trajectory prediction that achieves comparable accuracy to a 57M-parameter transformer while requiring no GPU and zero learned parameters. The method builds a transition table of historical state-to-next-position pairs and retrieves neighbors using a product kernel over spatial proximity, bearing, speed, and temporal context. Two inference modes operate over this shared representation: diversity-penalized sampling produces trajectories covering distinct plausible routes, while beam search finds the highest-likelihood path. On the TrAISformer benchmark (Danish Maritime AIS), our method achieves competitive accuracy at full data availability and dramatically outperforms the transformer in data-scarce regimes---remaining stable down to 10% of training data where TrAISformer degrades catastrophically. This enables deployment in new geographic regions from an order of magnitude less historical data, and with no GPU training.

</details>


### [158] [Mind the Long Tail: Understanding the Difficulty of Delay Detection in Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14367)
*Keyvan Amiri Elyasi,Lukas Kirchdorfer,Heiner Stuckenschmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过14个事件日志的分析揭示延迟检测的固有困难：剩余时间呈强右偏分布，现有模型对主流(众数)预测良好但对高延迟案例表现差，且存在明显的异方差性(延迟越大不确定性越高)。作者发现类别不平衡并非根本问题，并证明可利用不确定性与延迟的相关性显著提升延迟案例识别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测性流程监控(PPM)虽在剩余时间预测上取得进展，但现有研究多用聚合指标评估，无法揭示模型在目标分布上、尤其是在运营上最关键的高延迟案例上的表现，缺乏对延迟检测内在困难的理解。

Method: 在14个事件日志上系统分析剩余时间分布与模型行为：检验剩余时间的右偏性和高延迟案例占比；评估现有模型在不同延迟水平下的预测表现；揭示预测不确定性与延迟幅度的异方差关系；尝试缓解类别不平衡问题并评估其效果；最后利用不确定性与延迟的相关性来改进延迟案例的识别。

Result: 剩余时间普遍强右偏，仅少量案例具有大延迟；现有模型能较好捕捉分布的众数，但对高延迟案例表现差；存在显著异方差性，预测不确定性随延迟幅度增大而增加；缓解不平衡的方法收益有限，说明根本问题并非不平衡而是延迟案例本身的高不确定性；利用该相关性可显著提升延迟案例识别效果。

Conclusion: 研究阐明了延迟检测困难的根源在于延迟案例伴随的高不确定性，而非类别不平衡；不确定性感知建模是未来PPM研究的一个有前景的方向。

Abstract: The early detection of delayed cases in business processes is a critical capability for organizations. Predictive process monitoring (PPM) supports this task by using historical event logs to predict the remaining time of ongoing cases, enabling timely interventions to avoid missed deadlines and service level violations. Although remaining time prediction has advanced considerably through sophisticated deep learning architectures, little is known about the intrinsic difficulty of delay detection itself. Since performance is typically assessed using aggregate metrics, prior work provides limited insight into how models perform across the target distribution, especially on the operationally most critical cases with large delays. In this paper, we address this gap by analyzing the difficulty of delay detection. Across 14 event logs, we show that remaining times are typically strongly right-skewed, with only a small fraction of cases exhibiting large delays. Existing models capture the mode of this distribution well but perform poorly on high-delay cases. We further uncover pronounced heteroscedasticity, showing that predictive uncertainty increases with delay magnitude. Based on these findings, we evaluate approaches to mitigate the imbalance problem, but find only limited benefits, suggesting that the key underlying problem may not be imbalance but higher uncertainty associated with delayed cases. We show that this correlation can be exploited to substantially improve the identification of delayed cases. Overall, our work provides new insights into the sources of difficulty in delay detection and identifies uncertainty-aware modeling as a promising direction for future PPM research.

</details>


### [159] [Catching the Imposter: Self-Supervised Learning of Physical Coherence with Cross-Entity Feature Permutations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14372)
*Aleksei Rozanov,Arvind Renganathan,Vipin Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 imposter，一种利用物理一致性作为自监督信号的判别式预训练任务，并在 ERA5-Land 数据上系统比较了多种自监督目标在地表建模下游任务中的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学数据（如气候、地表变量）的实体特征受物理定律联合约束，但现有自监督学习目标大多忽略这种物理一致性，无法有效学习跨特征的物理依赖关系。

Method: 提出 imposter 判别式预训练任务：用另一个实体捐赠的真实观测值替换实体特征中的若干子集，训练编码器识别被替换的特征；由于每个捐赠值单独看都合理，模型只能通过跨特征物理依赖来求解。在 ERA5-Land 再分析数据的 21 个环境变量上进行预训练，并在统一架构与相同预训练预算下系统比较多种自监督目标。

Result: 在七类下游任务上评估（气候分类、碳通量估算、径流预测等），发现最优预训练任务取决于下游任务类别，而非某一目标的绝对优势；imposter 与现有自监督目标结合时可提供互补信息。

Conclusion: 物理一致性是科学基础模型有价值的新自监督信号来源，且不同下游任务需要不同的预训练目标组合。

Abstract: Scientific data often describe entities whose features are jointly governed by the laws of physics, yet existing self-supervised learning (SSL) objectives largely ignore this physical coherence. We introduce imposter, a discriminative pretext task that replaces subsets of an entity's features with real observations donated by another entity and trains the encoder to identify the swapped features. Because every donated value is individually plausible, the task can only be solved by learning cross-feature physical dependencies. We evaluate the proposed objectives on global ERA5-Land reanalysis data using 21 environmental variables and assess the learned representations on seven downstream tasks spanning climate classification, carbon flux estimation, and streamflow prediction. Our study includes, to our knowledge, the first systematic comparison of self-supervised objectives for land-surface modeling under a shared architecture and pre-training budget. We find that the most effective pretext task depends on the downstream task family rather than any single objective's superiority, and that imposter provides complementary information when combined with existing SSL objectives. These results suggest that physical coherence is a valuable new source of self-supervision for scientific foundation models.

</details>


### [160] [Boosting Data Augmentation with Stochastic Weight Averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14373)
*Longde Huang,Axel Flinth,Jan E. Gerken*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机权重平均（SWA）作为无需重复训练的集成替代方法，证明在无限宽度极限下，对增强数据使用 SWA 能获得超越其自身性能提升的等变性增益，并在计算机视觉和图分类等任务上验证了该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习任务的对称性是设计现代深度学习方案的重要因素，数据增强是引入对称性的常用方法。已有结果表明无限深的集成模型在增强数据上训练时会表现出完美对称性，但训练集成需要多次重复训练过程，成本高昂，因此需要一种无需重复训练的替代集成技术。

Method: 作者采用随机权重平均（SWA）作为集成替代方法，通过在训练结束时用 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 过程近似随机训练轨迹来分析 SWA，并研究其在无限宽度极限下对增强数据的行为。

Result: 在无限宽度极限下，对增强数据使用 SWA 能提供等变性增益（equiviariance boost），该增益超出了仅由 SWA 自身性能提升所能预期的效果。这一结论通过涵盖计算机视觉和图分类（含离散与连续对称性）的多种模型的大量数值实验得到验证。

Conclusion: SWA 作为一种无需重复训练的集成技术，不仅能提供集成带来的性能提升，还能在增强数据上额外获得等变性增益，是数据增强下高效利用对称性的有效方法。

Abstract: The symmetries of a learning task have become an important factor in designing modern deep learning solutions. Data augmentation is a straightforward and effective way of incorporating symmetries into a generic neural network. Recent results show that infinitely large deep ensembles show perfect symmetry when trained on augmented data. However, since training ensembles requires repeating the training process many times, this method is costly. In this work, we study stochastic weight averaging (SWA) as an alternative ensembling technique that does not require repeated training runs. We analyze SWA by approximating the stochastic training trajectory at the end of training with an Ornstein--Uhlenbeck process. We show that in the infinite-width limit, SWA on augmented data provides an equiviariance boost that goes beyond what could be expected from the performance increase due to SWA alone. We verify our results with extensive numerical experiments on numerous models spanning computer vision and graph classification with both discrete and continuous symmetries.

</details>


### [161] [DeaMoE: Efficient MoE Structure for Fast Small-Batch Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14385)
*Zewen Jin,Shen Fu,Zeping Duan,Shannon Wang,Weihao Wu,Chengjie Tang,Congkun Ai,Ping Gong,Zijian Dai,Youhui Bai,Cheng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeaMoE 是一种面向解码效率的 MoE 架构，通过将专家按专业领域分组为“部门”，让同部门专家共享大部分参数、仅保留少量私有参数，并配合定制两阶段路由策略，显著减少小批量解码时的专家权重加载量，从而缓解 MoE 推理的访存瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE 模型广泛用于编码助手、实时音视频交互等对响应延迟要求极高的场景。这些场景常采用小批量解码，此时 MoE 推理变为访存受限，专家权重加载成为严重瓶颈。现有方案（训练后权重压缩、预训练期间的细粒度专家设计）要么损害模型精度，要么引入额外的计算与通信开销，无法有效解决该问题。

Method: 提出 DeaMoE 架构：(1) 将专家按专业领域分组为若干“部门”（department），同部门专家因来自同一专业领域而共享大部分参数，同时每个专家保留少量私有参数以体现其独特性；(2) 设计定制的两阶段路由（two-stage routing）策略，避免冗余的专家权重加载，从而大幅提升 LLM 解码效率。

Result: 与 vanilla MoE 相比，DeaMoE 将每步加载的权重最多减少 50.9%，在 A40 上对预训练 7B 模型实现最高 1.33 倍的端到端 TPOT 加速；在微基准测试中，对 DeepSeek-V3 在 A40 和 H100 上分别达到最高 2.00 倍和 1.97 倍的峰值加速。

Conclusion: DeaMoE 通过“部门级参数共享 + 私有参数 + 两阶段路由”的组合，在不显著牺牲精度、不引入额外计算/通信开销的前提下，有效缓解了小批量解码场景下 MoE 推理的访存瓶颈，显著提升了推理效率。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models have been widely adopted in real-time interactive applications such as coding assistants, real-time audio-video interaction systems. To meet the extremely low response latency requirements of these scenarios, practitioners commonly employ small-batch decoding, under which MoE inference becomes memory-bound and is severely bottlenecked by expert weight loading. However, this bottleneck has received limited attention, and existing solutions such as post-training weight compression or fine-grained expert design during pre-training either degrade model accuracy or introduce additional computation and communication overhead. To tackle this issue, we propose DeaMoE, a decoding-efficient MoE architecture, in which the experts are grouped into several departments, and the experts belonging to the same department share most parameters since they come from the same professional field, and additionally each expert contains a few private parameters to reflect its uniqueness. Moreover, we design customized two-stage routing strategy for DeaMoE to avoid redundant loading, under which DeaMoE greatly improves the efficiency during LLM decoding. Compared with vanilla MoE, DeaMoE reduces per-step loaded weights by up to 50.9% and achieves up to 1.33 end-to-end TPOT speedup for the pre-trained 7B model on A40, and up to 2.00x and 1.97x peak speedup for DeepSeek-V3 on A40 and H100 in microbenchmarks.

</details>


### [162] [CytoBERT: A Foundation Model for Cytometry Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14414)
*Syed Abdul Haseeb Qadri,Bjarne C. Hiller,Felix Blanke,Vanja Sophie Cangalovic,Kutalmış Coşkun,Amin Mirzaei,Tom Siegl,Sebastian Bader,Thomas Kirste,Martin Becker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 CytoBERT，一个面向单细胞流式/质谱流式（cytometry）数据的开源、开放权重的自监督基础模型，通过标记标准化整合 15 个人类数据集、超过 5000 万个细胞进行预训练，学习可变标记面板下细胞内部标记间关系，并验证其在样本级分类任务上的跨数据集迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 细胞测量（cytometry）数据因实验方案和所选测量特征不同而高度异质、缺乏标准化，导致机器学习方法难以在不同研究间应用和迁移；该领域基础模型研究仍然稀缺，亟需一个能处理可变标记面板、具备跨研究泛化能力的基础模型。

Method: 提出 CytoBERT 基础模型：首先通过标记标准化（marker standardization）整合 15 个人类数据集、超 5000 万个细胞的大规模细胞测量语料库；随后以自监督方式在异质标记面板上进行预训练，使模型学习细胞内部跨标记的可迁移关系；再在样本级分类任务上进行微调以验证迁移学习效果。模型与代码均开源，代码托管于 GitHub。

Result: 在异质细胞测量数据集上进行样本级分类的微调实验表明，CytoBERT 能够实现跨数据集的迁移学习，证明在异质细胞测量数据上开展可扩展、可泛化分析是可行的，为后续研究提供了起点。

Conclusion: CytoBERT 作为首个面向可变标记面板单细胞细胞测量数据的开源基础模型，有效缓解了数据异质性和非标准化带来的挑战，展示了跨研究迁移的可行性，为可扩展、可泛化的细胞测量分析奠定了基础。

Abstract: Cytometry measures the complex characteristics of single cells (e.g., counts and protein expression of immune cells) and is widely used across immunological research and clinical settings. However, cytometry data is highly heterogeneous and unstandardized due to experimental protocols and the choice of measured features. While machine learning methods hold the potential to gain deeper insights into cell biology, these challenges make them difficult to apply and transfer across studies. Recent advances in foundation models can alleviate these issues, but corresponding approaches are still scarce in this field. To address this, we provide CytoBERT, a publicly available, open-source, open-weight foundation model for single-cell cytometry data with variable marker panels. CytoBERT is pretrained in a self-supervised manner on a large-scale cytometry corpus (15 human datasets with heterogeneous marker panels and more than 50 million cells) curated through marker standardization, enabling it to learn transferable inter-marker relationships within cells. Fine-tuning CytoBERT for sample-level classification demonstrates that transfer learning across heterogeneous cytometry datasets is feasible, providing a starting point for scalable, generalizable cytometry analysis. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [163] [More Correct Mass, Worse Answers: Why Power Sampling Can Fail and How to Fix It](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14420)
*Haohui Yang,Jiaxing Sun,Xiujun Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示Power Sampling的一个悖论：虽然它把更多概率质量推向正确轨迹，却会损害其本应增强的下游推理（如自一致性投票），准确率最多下降18.5个百分点。作者归因于剂量错配与覆盖错配，并提出一个变形控制、保持支持集的Power目标来修复，使其在同预算下超越标准多样本推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Power Sampling无需验证器即可在推理时锐化语言模型对完整轨迹的分布，有望成为众多下游采样方法的通用前端；但作者发现其实际反而会损害下游推理性能，亟需诊断原因并修复。

Method: 以自一致性(self-consistency)为代表性案例，跨模型与推理基准测量准确率下降，并识别出两类错配：(1)剂量错配——固定指数在不同问题上造成截然不同的分布变化量；(2)覆盖错配——全局锐化把质量集中于少量主导路径，即使高pass@k看似保有多样性，却丧失了聚合、搜索、选择所需的宽泛路径支持。据此提出变形控制、保持支持集的Power目标，逐问题校准锐化强度并抑制对中等概率路径的压制，再与加权自一致性在同预算下结合。

Result: 原始Power Sampling导致跨模型与基准最高18.5个百分点的准确率下降；修复后的采样器逆转了全局Power造成的损失，并在同预算的加权自一致性设置下超越标准多样本推理。

Conclusion: Power Sampling的全局均匀指数化存在剂量与覆盖双重错配，损害下游聚合推理；通过采用变形控制、保持支持集的Power目标进行校准，可在同预算下修复并超越标准多样本推理，验证了该诊断的有效性。

Abstract: Power Sampling sharpens a language model's distribution over complete generation trajectories, offering a verifier-free way to improve reasoning at inference time. It also has the potential to serve as a general-purpose front end for a broad range of downstream sampling methods. However, we uncover a striking paradox: Power Sampling can drive more probability mass toward correct trajectories while degrading the downstream inference it is intended to enhance. Using self-consistency as a representative case, we observe accuracy drops of up to 18.5 percentage points across models and reasoning benchmarks. We trace this paradox to two mismatches. Dose mismatch arises because a fixed exponent induces drastically different amounts of distributional change across problems. Coverage mismatch arises because global sharpening concentrates mass on a narrow set of dominant paths: high pass@k, often interpreted as evidence of preserved diversity, can therefore coexist with the loss of broad reasoning-path support required for downstream aggregation, search, and selection. Guided by this diagnosis, we replace uniform trajectory exponentiation with a deformation-controlled, support-preserving Power target that calibrates sharpening across problems while limiting the suppression of moderate-probability paths. In a same-budget instantiation with weighted self-consistency, the repaired sampler reverses the losses caused by global Power and outperforms standard multi-sample inference across reasoning benchmarks.

</details>


### [164] [Designing Compact Neural Architectures via Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14443)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种通用的双层优化框架用于神经架构搜索（NAS），通过连续松弛替代离散的神经元与激活函数决策，衍生出三种方法（NAS-NG、NAS-MA、NAS-NGMA），在MNIST和CIFAR-10上以更少参数取得竞争性或更优的准确率，并在CIFAR-10上优于vanilla DARTS。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NAS本质上是一个双层优化问题，但由于架构决策离散、搜索空间指数级增长以及候选架构训练成本高昂，导致计算开销巨大。作者希望通过将离散决策转化为连续松弛，实现可微分的架构搜索，从而规避组合搜索带来的可扩展性问题。

Method: 提出一个面向MLP、CNN、RNN、Transformer等多样架构的通用双层优化框架，并将离散的神经元级与激活函数级决策替换为连续松弛，形成三种可扩展的NAS方法：基于神经元门控的NAS（NAS-NG）、基于混合激活的NAS（NAS-MA），以及两者结合的NAS-NGMA。

Result: 在MNIST上，NAS-NGMA以7.69M MLP参数达到98.68%的测试准确率，NAS-NG以仅0.26M CNN参数达到99.63%的准确率；在CIFAR-10上，三种方法一致优于vanilla DARTS。此外，NAS-NG能够优化显著过参数化的及文献最优的架构，在提高准确率的同时减少参数量。

Conclusion: 松弛化的双层优化可作为离散NAS的可扩展替代方案，为神经元级和激活函数级的高效架构优化提供了一个通用框架，能够稳定识别紧凑且性能优异的架构。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) is naturally formulated as a bilevel optimization problem, where the upper-level optimizes the architecture using validation performance and the lower-level trains network parameters using training loss. However, NAS is computationally expensive due to discrete architectural decisions, exponentially growing search spaces, and the high cost of training candidate architectures. This work develops a general bilevel optimization framework for NAS across diverse architectures, including MLPs, CNNs, RNNs, and Transformers, to identify compact architectures with strong predictive performance. We propose three scalable formulations that replace discrete neuron- and activation-level decisions with continuous relaxations, enabling differentiable optimization over otherwise combinatorial architecture spaces. These formulations give rise to three NAS methods: NAS based on Neuron Gating (NAS-NG), NAS based on Mixed Activation (NAS-MA), and NAS based on Neuron Gating and Mixed Activation (NAS-NGMA). Experiments on MLPs and CNNs using MNIST and CIFAR-10 show that the proposed methods consistently identify compact architectures with competitive or improved predictive performance. On MNIST, NAS-NGMA achieves 98.68% test accuracy with 7.69M MLP parameters, while NAS-NG achieves 99.63% accuracy with only 0.26M CNN parameters. On CIFAR-10, the proposed methods consistently outperform vanilla DARTS. Further experiments demonstrate that NAS-NG can optimize substantially over-parameterized and literature-optimal architectures, improving accuracy while reducing parameters. These results establish relaxed bilevel optimization as a scalable alternative to discrete NAS and provide a general framework for efficient neuron- and activation-level architecture optimization.

</details>


### [165] [LP-NAS: Linear Programming-based Neural Architecture Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14472)
*Abhishek Shukla,Ankur Sinha,Faiz Hamid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 LP-NAS，一种基于线性规划的可微神经架构搜索框架。它利用验证损失梯度和训练损失 Hessian 构造线性规划来计算架构更新方向，在保持模型参数最优性的同时提升泛化能力。LP-NAS 在 DARTS 搜索空间上衍生出 S-LP-DARTS 和 R-LP-DARTS 两种高效变体，在 CIFAR-10/100 及 ImageNet 上表现出优于标准 DARTS 及多种变体的收敛速度和性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微 NAS 将离散的架构搜索空间连续化，从而可以借鉴连续优化方法；但现有可微 NAS 方法在搜索效率与泛化性能上仍有提升空间。作者希望将数学规划（线性规划）原理引入可微 NAS，利用验证损失与训练损失的梯度/Hessian 信息，设计能同时兼顾模型参数最优性和架构泛化能力的更高效搜索方法。

Method: 提出 LP-NAS 框架：将可微 NAS 的架构更新问题建模为线性规划（LP）。具体利用验证损失的梯度以及训练损失的 Hessian 构造线性规划，求解得到的架构更新方向在提升泛化（验证性能）的同时保持模型参数的最优性（满足训练损失的一阶最优性条件），并沿该 LP 导出的下降方向更新架构。作者给出两个计算高效变体 S-LP-NAS 和 R-LP-NAS，并将其应用于 DARTS 搜索空间，得到 S-LP-DARTS 和 R-LP-DARTS。

Result: 在 DARTS 搜索空间上，S-LP-DARTS 与 R-LP-DARTS 相比标准 DARTS 收敛更快，且搜索早期验证性能显著更高。在 CIFAR-10 和 CIFAR-100 上，LP-DARTS 在架构搜索阶段和评估阶段均优于标准 DARTS；与 P-DARTS、PC-DARTS、STO-DARTS 等 DARTS 变体在 CIFAR-10 上的对比也验证了其有效性。在 ImageNet 上的实验进一步验证了所发现架构的可迁移性。

Conclusion: LP-NAS 将线性规划成功引入可微 NAS，为连续搜索空间提供了通用且高效的架构更新机制。两种变体 S-LP-DARTS 和 R-LP-DARTS 在搜索速度、验证性能及架构可迁移性上均取得提升，表明数学规划方法在神经架构搜索中具有良好应用前景。

Abstract: Neural Architecture Search (NAS) aims to automate neural network architecture design, reducing reliance on human expertise. Among the various NAS methods, differentiable NAS has gained prominence due to its efficiency and accuracy compared to conventional NAS approaches. Since differentiable NAS relaxes the architecture search space into a continuous domain, it is possible to apply principles from continuous optimization to NAS. In this paper, we propose Linear Programming-based NAS (LP-NAS), a mathematical programming-based framework for differentiable NAS that is applicable to a wide range of continuous search spaces. LP-NAS formulates a linear program (LP) using the validation-loss gradient and the training-loss Hessian to compute an architecture update direction that improves generalization while preserving the optimality of the model parameters. By following this LP-derived descent direction, LP-NAS efficiently navigates the architecture search space, leading to faster and more effective architecture optimization. We introduce two computationally efficient variants of LP-NAS, namely S-LP-NAS and R-LP-NAS. Applying LP-NAS to the Differentiable Architecture Search (DARTS) search space results in two algorithmic variants, S-LP-DARTS and R-LP-DARTS. Both variants achieve faster convergence and significantly higher validation performance during the early search iterations than the standard DARTS algorithm. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10 and CIFAR-100 show that LP-DARTS outperforms standard DARTS in both the architecture search and evaluation phases. Additionally, we compare our approach with several DARTS variants (P-DARTS, PC-DARTS, and STO-DARTS) on the CIFAR-10 dataset and demonstrate its effectiveness. Furthermore, we validate the transferability of the discovered architectures through experiments on the ImageNet dataset.

</details>


### [166] [Approximate Muon with low-rank adapters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14492)
*Ben Anson,Conor Houghton,Edward Milsom*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 sMuon（small Muon），通过低秩设定下对松弛 Muon 目标做线性化与最小二乘近似，使 Muon 优化器能够与 LoRA 式低秩参数化结合，且实现仅用矩阵乘法（matmul），避免复杂线性代数分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon 优化器在预训练中优势明显，但很少用于参数高效微调（PEFT）；原因是最常见的 PEFT 方法 LoRA 的低秩参数化在数学上无法对权重更新做正交化，因而无法自然与 Muon 结合。

Method: 在低秩设定下对松弛后的 Muon 目标函数进行线性化，再用最小二乘法求解近似解；实现上仅依赖 matmul 运算，规避了更复杂的线性代数分解（如 SVD/QR 类）例程。

Result: sMuon 在 SFT（监督微调）和一个 ReLoRA 预训练实验中表现良好；结果虽依赖具体模型与评测任务，但总体表明将 Muon 用于低秩微调能带来中等程度的性能提升。

Conclusion: sMuon 成功弥合了 Muon 优化器与低秩 PEFT 之间的鸿沟，以纯 matmul 的高效实现取得有竞争力的性能，证明 Muon 式正交化思路可推广到低秩微调场景。

Abstract: The Muon optimizer shows clear benefits versus alternatives when pretraining neural networks. However, it is used less frequently for parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT). One potential reason is that the most common PEFT method, LoRA, does not naturally combine with Muon since it is not mathematically possible to orthogonalize the weight update given by a low-rank parameterization. In this paper, we address this issue by approximating the solution to a relaxed Muon objective in the low-rank setting via linearization and then least-squares. We provide an efficient implementation that uses matmul operations only, as opposed to more complex linear algebra decomposition routines. Our method, sMuon (small Muon), performs favourably across SFT and a ReLoRA pretraining experiment. While results are model- and eval-dependent, we find overall that using Muon for low-rank fine-tuning provides moderate performance improvements.

</details>


### [167] [Generating Benchmark Health Data Using a Tabular Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14496)
*Hao Yan,Lisa Pilgram,Dan Liu,Linglong Kong,Fida Dankar,Khaled El Emam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种两阶段跨表格数据生成（CTDG）框架：先将异构原始表统一转换为同构的标准化统计表（保留边缘分布与成对相关性），再用扩散Transformer学习并生成统计表，最后通过多元高斯采样和逆概率积分变换重建合成原始表。实验显示该方法在保真度与多样性上表现良好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成表格数据生成方法大多局限于单输入表场景，难以有效处理具有不同特征集的多个异构表格，因此需要一种能从多张异构表中学习统一生成模型并生成新合成表格数据的方法。

Method: 提出两阶段CTDG框架。第一阶段：将每张异构原始表转换为具有相同列集合的标准化统计表，每个统计表刻画原始各列的边缘分布及列间成对相关性。第二阶段：训练扩散Transformer模型以捕捉同构统计表之间的结构模式并生成合成统计表。最后，通过多元高斯采样和逆概率积分变换从生成的统计表重建出合成原始表。

Result: 实验结果表明，学习到的统计表示具有高保真度，生成的合成数据在保真度与多样性之间取得良好折衷，验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该两阶段CTDG框架能够从多个异构表中学习统一生成模型，支持生成不限数量的逼真合成异构表，并展现出高保真度的统计表示和良好的保真度-多样性平衡，证明了方法的有效性与实用性。

Abstract: Cross-Tabular Data Generation (CTDG) seeks to learn a generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and produce new synthetic tabular datasets. However, existing synthetic tabular data generation methods are largely restricted to single-input-table scenarios and struggle to effectively handle multiple heterogeneous tables with diverse feature sets. To address this limitation, we propose a two-stage framework for cross-tabular data generation. In the first stage, each heterogeneous raw table is transformed into a standardized statistical table with the same set of columns across all tables. Each statistical table captures the marginal distributions of the original columns and the pairwise correlations among them. In the second stage, a diffusion transformer model is trained to capture structural patterns across these homogeneous statistical tables and to generate synthetic statistical tables. Synthetic raw tables are subsequently reconstructed from the generated statistical tables via multivariate Gaussian sampling followed by an inverse probability integral transform. This two-stage CTDG framework enables the learning of a unified generative model from multiple heterogeneous tables and supports the generation of an unlimited number of realistic synthetic heterogeneous tables. Experimental results demonstrate high fidelity in the learned statistical representations and a favorable fidelity-diversity trade-off in the generated synthetic data, validating the effectiveness of the proposed approach.

</details>


### [168] [Rollplex: Cross-Phase GPU Spatial Sharing for Vision Language Model Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14498)
*Hanfeng Lu,Tianyu Feng,Suyi Li,Yuheng Zhao,Wei Gao,Shaopan Xiong,Ju Huang,Siran Yang,Jiamang Wang,Lin Qu,Wei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Rollplex 是一个面向视觉-语言模型（VLM）强化学习后训练的运行时系统，通过将前缀计算移入 rollout 解码窗口，实现参考/训练阶段与 rollout 的并行执行，在保持同步 RL 更新语义的同时显著提升 GPU 利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 on-policy RL 运行时将 rollout、参考评分、actor 训练按严格串行阶段执行。对 VLM 而言，处理稠密视频输入和 prompt 前缀占每个阶段很大比例，而前缀计算独立于生成的响应，因此可在 rollout 解码期间并行运行。串行执行导致 GPU 计算能力被严重浪费。

Method: Rollplex 将参考与训练阶段分解，并把前缀计算前移到 rollout 解码窗口内并行执行。为应对资源约束，提出两个关键机制：(1) 阶段感知的内存管理，依据生产者-消费者生命周期控制 HBM 驻留；(2) 并行度感知的权重共享，对不同 TP 并行度下布局兼容的张量复用同一物理存储，仅重建不兼容张量，从而避免完整的第二个 actor 副本。以 Qwen2.5-VL-32B 为例，朴素共置每 GPU 需约 165 GiB，且 rollout 与训练偏好不同的张量并行度和权重布局，上述机制有效缓解这些矛盾。

Result: 在 32 张 H800 GPU 上，Rollplex 比串行共置快 1.23–1.30 倍，比同等 GPU 预算下的分解执行快 1.57–2.24 倍，同时保持同步 RL 更新语义不变。

Conclusion: Rollplex 通过巧妙的调度分解与内存/权重管理机制，在不破坏同步 on-policy 语义的前提下，有效挖掘了 VLM RL 后训练中前缀计算与 rollout 解码之间的并行性，显著提升了训练吞吐效率。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) enable embodied agents to reason and act from visual observations and language instructions. Reinforcement learning (RL) post-training enhances these capabilities using task feedback, but current on-policy RL runtimes execute rollout, reference scoring, and actor training in strict serial phases. While effective for text-only RL, this phase-granular execution is wasteful for VLMs, where processing dense video inputs and prompt prefixes occupies a large fraction of each phase. Because prefix processing is independent of the generated response, it can be run alongside rollout decoding, which leaves GPU compute capacity underutilized, without breaking synchronous on-policy semantics.
  We present Rollplex, a runtime that decomposes the reference and training phase and moves the prefix computation into the rollout decode window. Realizing this schedule requires more than concurrent kernel launches: naive colocation of Qwen2.5-VL-32\,B requires roughly 165\,GiB per GPU, while rollout and training prefer different tensor-parallel (TP) degrees and weight layouts. Rollplex addresses these constraints with two mechanisms. Phase-aware memory management controls HBM residency according to producer--consumer lifetimes. Parallelism-aware weight sharing uses the same physical storage for layout-compatible tensors across distinct TP degrees and reconstructs only incompatible tensors, avoiding a complete second actor copy. On 32 H800 GPUs, Rollplex achieves $1.23\times$--$1.30\times$ speedup over serial colocation and $1.57\times$--$2.24\times$ over disaggregation under the same GPU budget, while preserving the synchronous RL update.

</details>


### [169] [RecipeNet: A Hierarchical Transformer for Recipe Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14505)
*Pin-Yen Huang,Sachin Chhabra,Prasanth Sai Gouripeddi,Abhinav Kumar,Baoxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 RecipeNet，一种分层 Transformer 架构，用于学习配方型结构化数据的表示，在多个数据集与任务上优于现有表格模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配方数据以有序步骤序列形式出现，每个步骤含异构结构化字段。现有表格学习方法将其扁平化为固定模式表示，丢失了字段间的层次交互与步骤间的过程依赖，因此需要能同时建模字段内层级与步骤序列依赖的表示学习方法。

Method: RecipeNet 采用分层 Transformer 架构：通过堆叠的 Transformer 编码器编码步骤内部的字段级交互，并编码跨步骤的顺序依赖，从而同时捕捉层次字段交互与过程顺序信息。

Result: 在多个配方数据集和任务上的实验表明，RecipeNet 一致地优于现有表格模型，验证了层次与序列建模对配方表示学习的价值。

Conclusion: RecipeNet 通过分层序列建模有效表征配方结构，说明同时考虑步骤内字段交互与步骤间顺序依赖能显著提升配方表示学习性能，为此类层次序列数据建模提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recipe data arises in domains such as materials synthesis, pharmaceutical formulation, and industrial manufacturing, where procedures are represented as ordered sequences of steps containing heterogeneous structured fields. Existing tabular learning methods typically flatten this structure into fixed-schema representations, limiting their ability to capture hierarchical field interactions and procedural dependencies. We propose RecipeNet, a hierarchical Transformer architecture that encodes field-level interactions within each step and sequential dependencies across steps through stacked Transformer encoders. Experiments on multiple recipe datasets and tasks demonstrate that RecipeNet consistently outperforms existing tabular models, highlighting the value of hierarchical and sequential modeling for recipe representation learning.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [170] [Learning Unsteady Aneurysm Hemodynamics with Physics-Informed DeepONets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13629)
*Oscar L. Cruz-Gonzalez,Valérie Deplano,Badih Ghattas*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种改进的多输入多输出物理信息DeepONet（M3PI-DeepONet），用于在理想化腹主动脉瘤几何中预测非定常3D血流，通过聚合注入策略实现输入自适应主干基函数，并结合3D Navier-Stokes方程进行物理约束训练，仅用0.3%内部标记数据即可同时预测速度与压力场（速度误差<4%，压力误差约5%），推断速度较CFD提升约36倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹主动脉瘤的临床决策需要患者特异性的血流动力学评估（壁面切应力、涡结构和压力分布），传统CFD计算成本高，而现有PI-DeepONet在处理复杂三维非定常流动时存在架构瓶颈，难以有效融合多物理约束并生成自适应的坐标基函数。

Method: 提出M3PI-DeepONet，核心创新为“聚合注入（Aggregated Injection）”策略：将多个输入分支的潜在表示在注入主干网络前进行融合，使坐标基能适应多种物理约束，实现输入自适应主干基。首次将逐层门控机制与多分支算子网络拓扑结合。以3D Navier-Stokes方程作为控制物理定律，基于物理信息残差、初始条件和边界条件训练，仅使用0.3%的内部标记数据和选定的分支条件信号。

Result: 在理想化AAA几何中，M3PI-DeepONet同时预测非定常3D速度场和压力场，平均相对L2速度误差低于4%，压力误差约5%；在分支条件输入可用的情况下，保守估计的保留周期推断速度相比参考CFD仿真提升约36倍。

Conclusion: 该工作推动了深度学习在心血管疾病建模中的应用，是迈向实时、非侵入式临床诊断的一步，证明了结合物理约束的多分支算子网络在复杂3D血流预测中的潜力。

Abstract: Clinically actionable, patient-specific hemodynamic assessment, specifically wall shear stress, vortex structure and pressure distributions, is critical for determining risky or unfavorable evolution in Abdominal Aortic Aneurysms (AAA). While Physics-Informed Deep Operator Networks (PI-DeepONets) show promising results in complementing established 5 tools such as Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD), a persistent architectural challenge remains for complex 3D flows. In this direction, we propose a Modified Multi-Input Multi-Output PI-DeepONets (M3PI-DeepONet) designed for predicting unsteady flows in an idealized AAA geometry. Central to our model is the Aggregated Injection strategy, where latent representations from multiple input branches are fused prior to trunk injection, allowing the coordinate basis to adapt to multiple physical constraints. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first architecture to combine the layer-wise gating mechanism with a multi-branch operator-network topology, yielding an input-adaptive trunk basis. Additionally, we integrate the 3D Navier-Stokes equations as governing physical laws, so the model is trained based on physics-informed residuals, initial and boundary conditions, and only 0.3% of the labeled internal data together with the selected branch-conditioning signals. The M3PI-DeepONet simultaneously predicts unsteady 3D flow velocity and pressure fields with an average relative L2 velocity error below 4% and pressure error around 5% while achieving a conservative retained-cycle inference speedup of approximately 36x compared to reference CFD simulations once the branch inputs used for conditioning are available. This work advances the application of deep learning in cardiovascular disease modeling, marking step toward real-time, non-invasive clinical diagnostics.

</details>


### [171] [On the Brittleness of Maximum Likelihood Estimation for Gaussian Process Hyperparameter Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13793)
*Tyler R. Johnson,Kian Ben-Jacob,Christopher P. Muller,Ramin Bostanabad*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了最大似然估计（MLE）在训练高斯过程（GP）用于概率回归和分类任务时的脆弱性，并将其与理论有据的度量指标进行对比，提出了实用解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MLE 虽然流行、有效且直观，但在其前提假设不满足时表现脆弱，会导致 GP 泛化能力下降；而 GP 在工程设计中被广泛使用，常被（错误地）认为对过拟合非常鲁棒。

Method: 对用于概率回归/分类任务的 GP，以 MLE 为基准，对照理论有据的度量指标进行根本性评估，并提出实用的训练/选择解决方案。

Result: 大量实验表明所提方案在下游设计任务（如贝叶斯优化）中有效，并能构建出在预测精度、不确定性量化和推理成本上甚至优于表格基础模型（TabPFN）的 GP。

Conclusion: MLE 训练 GP 具有脆弱性；采用理论有据的替代方案可提升 GP 的准确性与鲁棒性，并为从业者提供了构建高性能 GP 的蓝图，相关代码已开源。

Abstract: Machine learning (ML) has become an indispensable part of modern engineering design workflows. A crucial step in training an ML model is the selection of the loss function which can be systematically formulated via various techniques such as maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) and cross-validation . While MLE is one of the most popular, effective, and intuitive mechanisms for training ML models, it is brittle: if the assumptions underpinning it are not met, the trained ML model may generalize poorly. This brittleness affects even Gaussian processes (GPs) which are widely used in engineering design and are often (incorrectly) presumed to be very robust to overfitting. In this paper, we fundamentally evaluate the brittleness of MLE in the context of training GPs for probabilistic regression or classification tasks. We compare theoretically grounded metrics against MLE and propose practical solutions. Our extensive studies demonstrate the effectiveness of our solutions in downstream design tasks such as Bayesian optimization and provide a blueprint for practitioners to build accurate and robust GPs that can even outperform tabular foundation models in terms of prediction accuracy, uncertainty quantification, and inference cost. Our contributions are publicly available via GitHub at https://github.com/Bostanabad-Research-Group/GP-vs-TabPFN-vs-GPyTorch.

</details>


### [172] [Deep Vision in Smart Manufacturing: MODERN Framework for Intelligent Quality Monitoring and Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13937)
*Yicheng Kang,Yuling Jiao,Xin Geng,Mahesh Nagarajan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出MODERN框架，基于Inception残差神经网络实现工业质量监控与故障隔离，通过缺陷似然控制图、迁移学习故障定位及迁移监控方法，在理论与实证上均优于现有方法，并得出"持续升级监控设备未必最优"的管理启示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能制造环境部署了大量传感器、成像设备和计算机，为即时通信与智能管理提供了可能，但传统工业质量控制未能充分整合这些能力，尤其在训练数据不足时缺乏有效的质量监控与故障隔离手段。

Method: 本文提出MODERN深度学习框架：(1) 基于Inception残差神经网络架构构建监控产品缺陷似然的控制图；(2) 利用迁移学习构建故障区域估计器以定位缺陷区域；(3) 针对训练数据不足情形，提出仅需小样本的迁移监控技术，并采用假设检验方法定量评估方法适用性。

Result: 理论上建立了缺陷似然估计与故障诊断的minimax最优收敛率；实验上通过模拟实验与真实数据表明，该方法在性能上优于现有最先进方法；并推导出一个看似反直觉的管理启示——不计成本地持续升级监控设备并不总是符合制造商的最佳利益。

Conclusion: MODERN框架有效整合深度学习与智能制造能力，在缺陷监测与故障隔离上兼具理论最优性和实证优越性。研究揭示设备升级存在收益递减，制造商应权衡升级成本，而非盲目持续投资监控设备。

Abstract: Smart manufacturing processes are often installed with a large number of sensors, imaging devices and computers, which not only enable instant communication across various modules of a production system but also aid in intelligent manufacturing management. In this paper, we introduce MODERN, a deep learning framework for quality monitoring and fault isolation, which integrates these enhanced capabilities into the practice of industrial quality control. Using the architecture of an inception residual neural network, we develop a control chart that monitors the likelihood of a product containing defects. We also propose a faulty region estimator that identifies the defective area using transfer learning. To extend our framework to cases where there are not sufficient training data, we suggest a transfer monitoring technique that requires only a small sample size and a hypothesis testing approach for quantitatively assessing the applicability of our method. Theoretically, we establish the minimax optimal convergence rate for both our defect likelihood estimation and fault diagnosis. Our results lead to a seemingly counter-intuitive managerial implication - it may not always be in a manufacturer's best interests to keep upgrading its monitoring equipment regardless of the cost. Empirically, we demonstrate the superior performance of our method in comparison with a state-of-the-art approach using both simulated experiments and real data.

</details>


### [173] [Extending Occam's inversion with lasso fusion, overcomplete dictionaries, and isotropic total variation regularisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14225)
*Anandaroop Ray*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在Occam反演框架下系统阐述了l1正则化地球物理反演，澄清了多维全变分（TV）的方向不变性问题，统一了合成/分析两种lasso融合形式，并解析、比较了坐标下降、IRLS与分裂Bregman三种求解器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Occam反演传统上使用l2模型粗糙度，产生平滑模型；l1正则化能给出视觉上更锐利的模型，但地球物理中的l1反演与统计与成像文献脱节，尤其多维TV的两种形式（各向异性/各向同性，仅后者边缘方向不变）常被混淆。作者希望厘清数学基础并纳入统一的Occam框架，推动地球物理界采用l1反演。

Method: 在Occam框架下取l2数据范数与l1模型正则化，证明一维时合成与分析的lasso融合归结为同一问题（即fused lasso）；将框架扩展到多维及字典中的小波变换等算子；解析并采用三种常用求解器——坐标下降、迭代重加权最小二乘（IRLS）和分裂Bregman——求解线性（1D回归、2D去模糊）与非线性（1D航空瞬变电磁，含实测数据）问题序列。

Result: 表明一维合成与分析lasso融合等价；给出多维TV方向不变性的区分；在测试问题上比较三种求解器，IRLS相比分裂Bregman所需最小二乘求解次数最多可少一个数量级。

Conclusion: 推荐一维问题使用坐标下降，二维问题优先使用IRLS（而非分裂Bregman）。通过将l1正则化的各种形式呈现为熟悉的Occam反演，希望进一步鼓励地球物理学家采用l1正则化反演。

Abstract: Occam's inversion is a robust algorithm to perform nonlinear geophysical inversion. It provides the smoothest model within observation noise, thereby discouraging geological overinterpretation. While Occam originally penalised l2 model roughness, l1 can be used to provide models that are visually sharp. However, l1 regularised geophysical inversion has diverged from the larger body of statistics and imaging literature. For example, l1 regularisation with a difference operator (i.e., total variation) in multiple dimensions has two distinct forms, only one of which is invariant to edge orientation -- a distinction often overlooked in geophysics. In one dimension this reduces to the fused lasso problem in statistics. Within the Occam framework, for l2 data norm and l1 model regularisation, we show that lasso fusion through either synthesis or analysis leads to the same 1D problem. We extend the framework to multiple dimensions and to operators such as wavelet transforms in a dictionary, unravelling the mathematics behind three commonly used solvers for l1 regularised problems. These are coordinate descent, iteratively reweighted least squares (IRLS), and split Bregman. We use them to solve a sequence of problems that are linear (1D regression, 2D deblurring) and nonlinear (1D airborne transient electromagnetics) including a field data example. We recommend coordinate descent for 1D problems, and IRLS over split Bregman for 2D problems, with IRLS requiring up to an order of magnitude fewer least squares solves. We hope this work will further encourage geophysicists to adopt l1 regularised inversion, by presenting its various forms as a familiar Occam's inversion.

</details>


### [174] [Offline Deep Q* Estimation with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14401)
*Xiaohong Chen,Yuling Jiao,Lican Kang,Jerry Zhijian Yang,Chen Zhong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将算子估计与值函数学习解耦的离线强化学习框架，用条件扩散模型估计奖励律和转移核以近似最优Bellman算子，再通过最小化经验Bellman残差训练$Q^*$的深度估计器，并在无完备性假设下给出尖锐的非渐近收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线强化学习中估计最优动作值函数$Q^*$的本质是仅基于离线观测求解最优Bellman方程；核心困难在于奖励函数与转移核未知，导致最优Bellman算子无法直接从数据观测。现有深度RL理论常依赖完备性假设，本文旨在摆脱该假设，并给出端到端的理论保证。

Method: 提出解耦框架：第一阶段用条件扩散模型估计奖励律与转移核，诱导出最优Bellman算子的数据驱动近似；第二阶段将这些估计量代入Bellman方程，在神经网络函数类上最小化经验Bellman残差，得到$Q^*$的深度估计器。理论上先用全变差距离对条件扩散估计做端到端分析，得到学习最优Bellman算子的尖锐收敛率，再在集中性条件下将残差界转化为$L^2$收敛率。

Result: 给出值阶段超额Bellman残差风险的oracle界$\widetilde{\mathcal O}(n^{-\frac{2\beta}{d_x+d_a+2\beta}})$，并在集中性条件下得到$Q^*$深度估计器的$L^2$收敛率$\widetilde{\mathcal O}(n^{-\frac{\beta}{d_x+d_a+2\beta}})$，其中$d_x$、$d_a$为状态与动作空间维度，$\beta$为$Q^*$的Hölder光滑指数。理论分析不依赖深度RL常用的完备性假设，数值实验验证了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架通过扩散模型估计算子与值函数学习的解耦，在无需完备性假设的条件下同时实现理论与实证上的良好表现，为离线RL中最优值函数的估计提供了新的方法论与理论保证。

Abstract: In offline RL, estimating the optimal action-value function $Q^*$ can be formulated as solving the optimal Bellman equation based solely on offline observations. A fundamental challenge is that the reward function and transition kernel are unknown, so the optimal Bellman operator is not directly observable from data. To address this issue, we propose a novel framework that decouples operator estimation from value function learning. In this approach, we first formulate conditional diffusion models to estimate the reward law and transition kernel, which induces a data-driven approximation of the optimal Bellman operator. We then plug these estimators into the Bellman equation and obtain a deep estimator of $Q^*$ by minimizing the empirical Bellman residual over a neural network function class. Theoretically, we first establish sharp nonasymptotic convergence rates for learning the optimal Bellman operator through an end-to-end analysis of conditional diffusion estimation in total variation distance. We then establish the oracle value-stage rate $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\bigl(n^{-\frac{2β}{d_x+d_a+2β}}\bigr)$ for the excess Bellman residual risk. Finally, under a concentrability condition, we translate this residual bound into an $L^2$ convergence rate of $\widetilde{\mathcal O}\bigl(n^{-\fracβ{d_x+d_a+2β}}\bigr)$ for the resulting deep estimator of $Q^*$, where $d_x$ and $d_a$ denote the dimensions of the state and action spaces, respectively, and $β$ denotes the Hölder smoothness index of $Q^*$. Importantly, our theoretical analysis does not rely on completeness assumptions commonly used in deep RL theory. Extensive numerical experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method and its strong empirical performance.

</details>


### [175] [Online Inference in Distributional Temporal-Difference Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14408)
*Yang Peng,Liangyu Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究固定策略下回报分布泛函的在线统计推断，利用非参数分布时序差分学习从单条马尔可夫轨迹估计回报分布，并为Polyak–Ruppert平均估计量建立了中心极限定理与bootstrap一致性，从而支持光滑及非光滑（如分位数）统计泛函的推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习中，对固定策略下回报分布的统计推断（而不仅是点估计）对风险评估和决策至关重要。现有分布时序差分学习多关注估计一致性，缺乏在单条轨迹下严格刻画估计量渐近分布与推断方法（尤其是bootstrap）的理论，难以对诸如CVaR、期望损失、分位数等风险泛函进行有效的置信区间构造。

Method: 采用非参数分布时序差分学习（distributional temporal-difference learning）从单一马尔可夫轨迹估计回报分布；对Polyak–Ruppert平均估计量建立弱收敛理论，证明其root-T误差在Cramér空间弱收敛于中心化高斯随机元。接着证明在给定观测轨迹的条件下，bootstrap与原始平均量之差的root-T同样弱收敛到同一高斯极限。对光滑统计泛函直接应用函数型delta方法；对非光滑泛函，构建回报CDF在有限多个阈值T^{-1/2}邻域上的局部渐近理论及其bootstrap版本，用于处理由CDF方程刻画的统计量（如分位数）。

Result: 证明Polyak–Ruppert平均估计量的root-T误差在Cramér空间弱收敛于中心化高斯随机元；条件于观测轨迹，bootstrap与原始平均量之差的root-T弱收敛到相同高斯极限。由此为光滑泛函（方差、CVaR、期望损失、expectiles）提供bootstrap推断保证；并为非光滑泛函（回报分位数等）建立了CDF在阈值附近的局部渐近理论及对应bootstrap理论，使基于CDF方程的推断可行。

Conclusion: 该工作为固定策略下回报分布泛函的在线统计推断奠定了完整理论基础：既覆盖光滑统计泛函的正态逼近与bootstrap一致性，也覆盖非光滑泛函的局部渐近分析，显著拓展了分布强化学习在统计推断与风险量化方面的适用性。

Abstract: We study online statistical inference for functionals of the return distribution under a fixed policy. The return distribution is estimated by nonparametric distributional temporal-difference learning from a single Markov trajectory. For the Polyak--Ruppert averaged estimator, we prove that its root-$T$ error converges weakly to a centered Gaussian random element in Cramér space. We also prove that, conditionally on the observed trajectory, the root-$T$ difference between the bootstrap and original averages converges weakly to the same Gaussian limit. These results justify bootstrap inference for smooth statistical functionals, including variance, CVaR, expected shortfall, and expectiles. For nonsmooth statistical functionals, we develop a local asymptotic theory for the estimated return CDF over $T^{-1/2}$-neighborhoods of finitely many thresholds, together with its bootstrap analogue. This theory allows us to conduct inference for nonsmooth statistical functionals characterized by CDF equations, including return quantiles.

</details>


<div id='stat.ME'></div>

# stat.ME [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [Bias Reduction for Local Polynomial Derivative Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13672)
*Fujia Chang,W. John Braun*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种迭代数据锐化方法，用于降低非参数回归中局部线性导数估计的偏差，在保留局部线性拟合简单性的同时将偏差阶从 O(h²) 逐级降至 O(h^{2l+2})。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 局部多项式平滑是非参数回归的常用工具，但局部线性导数估计仍存在 O(h²) 阶的偏差。作者希望在不牺牲局部线性拟合简洁性的前提下，进一步降低导数估计的偏差。

Method: 方法基于两个期望算子：作用于回归函数的 L_0 和作用于一阶导数的 L_1。通过反复施加残差算子 R=I-L_0，构造一系列锐化后的导数估计。经 l 步锐化后，偏差阶由 O(h²) 降至 O(h^{2l+2})。对高斯核，所有锐化系数均为 1，得到简洁的单带宽闭式表达式。

Result: 在三个光滑测试函数上的模拟实验表明，该方法能显著降低估计偏差，同时揭示出偏差-方差之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 所提出的迭代数据锐化方法能有效降低局部线性导数估计的偏差，且对高斯核具有简洁闭式形式，为实现更精确的非参数导数估计提供了实用途径，其代价是方差相应增加。

Abstract: Local polynomial smoothing is commonly used in non-parametric regression, but local linear derivative estimation still has a bias of order $O(h^2)$. This paper proposes an iterative data sharpening method to reduce the bias of derivative estimates while retaining the simplicity of local linear fitting. The method is based on two expectation operators: $L_0$, acting on the regression function, and $L_1$, acting on the first-order derivative. By repeatedly applying the residual operator $R=I-L_0$, a series of sharpened derivative estimates can be constructed. After $l$ sharpening steps, the bias order can be reduced from $O(h^2)$ to $O(h^{2l+2})$. For the Gaussian kernel, all sharpening coefficients equal 1, giving a simple closed-form single-bandwidth expression. Simulation experiments on three smooth test functions show that this method can significantly reduce the estimation bias while revealing a bias-variance trade-off.

</details>


### [177] [Nonprobability Samples for Small Area Estimation: A Review and Comparative Simulation Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13673)
*Sho Kawano,Daniel Vedensky,Qianyu Dong,Ethan Pawl,Qi Wang,Paul A. Parker,Zehang Richard Li,Scott Harold Holan*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文回顾了非概率样本（NPS）方法的最新进展，引入数据缺陷相关（DDC）作为数据质量度量与方法分类工具，并通过综合模拟研究在不同DDC水平下评估多种NPS方法，同时将若干现有方法扩展到小域估计（SAE）情境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非概率样本因成本低、样本量大且能触达传统概率调查无法覆盖的人群而日益受青睐；随着传统调查应答率下降，NPS在小域估计中需求猛增。但方法虽发展迅速，却缺乏对不同条件下何种方法表现最佳的清晰认识。

Method: 回顾NPS方法学最新进展，引入数据缺陷相关（DDC）作为衡量数据质量和分类各类NPS方法的工具；开展综合模拟研究，在不同DDC水平下评估多种NPS方法，并将若干现有方法扩展至小域估计（SAE）场景。

Result: 通过模拟研究系统地比较了不同DDC水平下各类NPS方法的表现，并展示了扩展至SAE的若干方法的效果（论文为综述与模拟研究，具体数值结果需见正文）。

Conclusion: NPS方法在小域估计中具有巨大潜力，DDC可作为评估数据质量与选择方法的有用框架；不同条件下方法表现各异，需要依据DDC水平等方法学特征审慎选择合适的方法。

Abstract: Nonprobability samples (NPS) are attractive because they are less costly to collect, can provide substantially larger sample sizes, and may reach populations that traditional probability surveys do not. As response rates for traditional surveys fall, interest in NPS has grown rapidly within the field of survey statistics. These methods are especially relevant for small area estimation (SAE), where there is ever-present demand for estimates at fine geographic scales and detailed demographic domains. Despite rapid methodological development, there remains limited understanding of which approaches perform best under different conditions. In this paper, we review recent developments in NPS methodology, including the concept of data defect correlation (DDC) as a measure of data quality and as a tool for categorizing the various NPS methods. We then present a comprehensive simulation study that evaluates a range of NPS approaches under varying levels of DDC and extend several existing methods to the SAE setting.

</details>


### [178] [Hyperbolic Latent Position Models for Hierarchical Bipartite Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13713)
*Kisung You*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出HELPI模型，将二分图数据的行与列对象同时嵌入双曲空间，分离几何交互与加性倾向，用有根Gromov积刻画共享祖先层级结构，并通过变分推断实现估计，在Junyi Academy与美国众议院投票数据上验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二分图数据（如学生答题、议员投票）同时具有行/列异质性与层级交互结构。欧氏空间体积随半径仅多项式增长，无法匹配分支层级的指数扩张特性；而双曲空间体积指数增长，其有根Gromov积能度量共享祖先深度，天然契合这种层级结构。因此需要一种在双曲空间中建模二分交互数据的潜伏位置模型。

Method: 提出HELPI（HypErbolic Latent Position model for bipartite Interaction data）模型：将两类对象同时嵌入双曲空间，把几何交互项与加性倾向（主效应）分离。在无限制主效应下，双曲距离预测子与有根Gromov积预测子在似然上等价；径向深度被加性项吸收，留下根不变的投影层级信号作为可识别的几何目标。作者建立了可识别性与后验收缩理论结果，并针对二值结果开发了增广变分推断程序。

Result: 模拟实验验证了模型对层级交互的恢复能力，并厘清了弱分支情形下的表现。在Junyi Academy数据中，课程锚点产生可解释的几何结构，时间预测接近项目反应理论（IRT）基准，并能区分"尝试过的练习"与"无条件掌握"；在美国众议院唱名投票数据中，灵活的HELPI变体优于加性模型与双参数逻辑斯蒂（2PL）基准，且在不使用党派标签拟合的情况下恢复了党派与政策结构。

Conclusion: HELPI利用双曲几何的指数扩张特性与有根Gromov积的共享祖先度量，成功建模二分交互数据中的层级结构，兼具理论保证（可识别性、后验收缩）、计算可行性（增广变分推断）与实证有效性（预测基准、可解释的结构恢复），为带层级交互的二分网络分析提供了新的几何框架。

Abstract: Binary bipartite data often combine row and column heterogeneity with hierarchical interaction, as when students answer exercises organized by prerequisites or legislators vote on nested policy areas. Fixed-dimensional Euclidean volume grows only polynomially with radius, whereas branching hierarchies expand exponentially. Hyperbolic space matches this growth, and its rooted Gromov product measures shared ancestry. We propose the \emph{HypErbolic Latent Position model for bipartite Interaction data} (HELPI), which places both object types in hyperbolic space while separating geometric interaction from additive propensities. With unrestricted main effects, a hyperbolic-distance predictor is likelihood-equivalent to a rooted Gromov-product predictor. Radial depth is absorbed by additive terms, leaving a root-invariant projected hierarchy signal as the identified geometric target. We establish identification and posterior contraction results and develop augmented variational procedures for binary outcomes. Simulations support recovery of hierarchical interaction and clarify weak-branch regimes. In Junyi Academy data, curriculum anchors yield an interpretable geometry with temporal prediction close to item-response benchmarks, while distinguishing attempted exercises from unconditional mastery. In U.S. House roll calls, flexible HELPI variants improve on additive and two-parameter logistic benchmarks and recover party and policy structure without using party labels during fitting.

</details>


### [179] [Detection of Structural Distortions in Functional Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13762)
*Debanjana Datta,Rituparna Sen,Nalini Ravishanker*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文从贝叶斯视角出发，针对函数型时间序列的结构突变检测问题，提出多种新颖方法，通过状态空间建模和分块吉布斯采样，同时识别函数序列中个别及同时发生的局部结构畸变位置，并在金融与温度数据上验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着高维和函数型数据的激增，结构突变检测需求上升。与经典单变量时间序列不同，函数型数据的结构变化不必全域同时发生，而可能局部出现并具有稀疏性；且事前不知哪些参数发生转变，单个成分信噪比低，需要跨维度聚合信息以检测全局变化。

Method: 本文采用函数型时间序列的状态空间表示来构建问题，开发多种新颖贝叶斯方法以捕捉随机函数序列中固有且可能同时发生的结构畸变，并提出高效的分块吉布斯采样（Blocked Gibbs Sampling）算法以准确识别突变位置。

Result: 所提方法能够准确识别函数型时间序列中局部结构突变的位置，并在多个金融与温度数据集上展示了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的贝叶斯状态空间框架结合分块吉布斯采样，能有效检测函数型时间序列中稀疏、局部化的结构变动，较传统单变量方法更好地适应高维函数型数据，并为金融和气温等实际数据提供了可靠的结构突变识别工具。

Abstract: In the era of modern data science, the rapid proliferation of high-dimensional and functional datasets has fostered increasing interest in the investigation of paradigm shifts and structural breaks.
  Unlike classical univariate time series, structural changes in functional data need not occur simultaneously across the entire domain; instead, they may emerge locally, producing heterogeneous distortions across the underlying functional structure. The patterns of instability often exhibit sparsity, where it is not known \textit{a priori} which specific parameters are undergoing a transition. However, in functional contexts, these shifts are often "localised". The difficulty lies in the high dimensionality of the parameter space, where the signal-to-noise ratio may be low for individual components, necessitating the aggregation of information across dimensions to detect a global change. This paper addresses the problem of detecting structural shifts in a functional time series from a Bayesian perspective. We have developed various novel methodologies that capture the inherent structural distortion in a sequence of random functions, both individually and simultaneously. The formulation of the problem is based on the state-space representation of a functional time series. Efficient Blocked Gibbs Sampling algorithms have been proposed to identify these locations accurately. Further, we demonstrate the effectiveness of our methods on several financial and temperature datasets.

</details>


### [180] [Separating Spatial and Clinical Risk with Node-Splitting SVM Survival Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13847)
*Drew Lazar,Aye Aye Maung*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种非参数两阶段方法，用于在存在空间自相关预后协变量的情况下，将空间风险与患者临床特征相分离，恢复地理差异中的生存信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恢复生存的地理变异需要将空间风险与患者临床特征分开，但预后协变量本身常具有空间结构，导致临床变异与空间变异相互混杂、难以辨识。作者需要一种不预设临床风险函数形式、且能产出带有锐利（可能弯曲）边界的分段常数空间风险图的方法。

Method: 采用非参数两阶段流程：(1) 仅用协变量拟合一棵临床生存树，取其叶节点的 Nelson-Aalen 累积风险残差，将删失生存结构转换到"经过临床调整"的尺度上，而不对临床风险施加任何函数形式；(2) 对这些残差在空间坐标上拟合第二棵树，恢复空间结构。两阶段均使用核偶极子分裂（kernel dipole-splitting）生存树，所得空间风险图分段常数、边界锐利且可弯曲。理论上，在乘法脆弱项与外生性条件下证明残差在给定位置后与临床协变量无关，并随脆弱项随机有序；通过对脆弱项 Laplace 指数的展开量化残差的近似指数性并识别主导余项。

Result: 在 LeukSurv 白血病数据上，本方法与贝叶斯高斯随机场脆弱项模型在"风险升高位置"上达成一致，同时能分辨出平滑曲面所平均掉的锐利邻接结构；而未做临床调整的纯空间分析会把临床变异错误归因于地理位置。在分区已知的模拟中（包括对外生性逐步违背的扫描），该方法定位出两类贡献不再可分离的临界点，平滑基准方法在接近该点的过程中同步退化。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种稳健的两阶段拆解手段，既能去除空间结构化的临床混杂、又保留空间风险的锐利边界，且在理论上有残差恢复空间信号的保证，其构造可推广到其他生存估计器。

Abstract: Recovering geographic variation in survival requires separating spatial risk from patients' clinical characteristics, a problem complicated by prognostic covariates that are themselves spatially structured. We develop a nonparametric two-stage method for this separation. A clinical survival tree fit to the covariates alone supplies leaf Nelson-Aalen cumulative hazard residuals, transferring the censored survival structure to a clinically adjusted scale without imposing a functional form on the clinical hazard, and a second tree fit to these residuals on the coordinates recovers the spatial structure. Both stages are kernel dipole-splitting survival trees, so the resulting spatial risk map is piecewise constant, with sharp, possibly curved boundaries. We establish when the residuals recover the spatial signal: under a multiplicative frailty and an exogeneity condition, they are free of the clinical covariates given location and stochastically ordered by the frailty. An expansion of the frailty Laplace exponent quantifies their approximate exponentiality and identifies the leading remainder. These results assume consistency of the first stage rather than a model class, so the construction extends to other survival estimators. On the LeukSurv leukemia data the method agrees with a Bayesian Gaussian random field frailty about where risk is elevated while resolving sharp adjacencies the smooth surface averages away, and an unadjusted spatial analysis misattributes clinical variation to location. Simulations with known zones, including a sweep through graded violations of exogeneity, locate the point at which the two contributions cease to be separately identifiable, with the smooth benchmark degrading in parallel as that point is approached.

</details>


### [181] [A semiparametric approach for the estimation of covariate-adjusted area under the receiver operating characteristic curve](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13859)
*Shanshan Liu,Jianlei Huang,Guoqing Diao*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于广义可加模型（GAM）的半参数框架，用于估计随协变量变化的AUC（covariate-specific）及协变量校正的AUC（covariate-adjusted），可处理二分类和多分类疾病状态，并证明了估计量的渐近性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ROC曲线与AUC广泛用于评估生物标志物的判别能力，但在许多临床场景中，诊断准确性会随患者特征显著变化；若忽视这种异质性，可能导致误导性结论。因此需要一种能够刻画协变量对生物标志物性能非线性与交互影响的估计方法。

Method: 提出新的半参数框架，基于广义可加模型（GAM）估计协变量特异性（covariate-specific）和协变量校正（covariate-adjusted）的AUC；允许协变量对生物标志物性能的非线性效应和交互效应；同时支持二分类与多分类疾病状态；并建立所提估计量的渐近性质。

Result: 模拟研究表明该方法具有良好的有限样本表现；在阿尔茨海默病神经影像学倡议（ADNI）数据中，观察到生物标志物判别能力随协变量存在显著异质性。

Conclusion: 该方法能够有效捕捉协变量对生物标志物判别能力的影响，在存在异质性的临床数据中提供比传统AUC更准确、稳健的评价，具有广泛的应用价值。

Abstract: Receiver operating characteristic (ROC) and the area under the ROC curve (AUC) are widely used to evaluate the discriminative ability of biomarkers. In many clinical settings, however, diagnostic accuracy varies substantially across patient characteristics, and failure to account for such heterogeneity can lead to misleading conclusions. We propose a new semiparametric framework based on generalized additive models to estimate covariate-specific and covariate-adjusted AUC while allowing for nonlinear and interaction effects of covariates on biomarker performance. Our method accommodates both binary and multicategory disease status. We also establish the asymptotic properties of the proposed estimators. Simulations demonstrate favorable finite-sample performance. We illustrate the method using data from the Alzheimer's Disease Neuroimaging Initiative, where substantial heterogeneity in biomarker discrimination across covariates is observed.

</details>


### [182] [Change-Point Detection for Heterogeneous High-Dimensional Functional Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13880)
*Xufei Tang,Dan Zhuang,Houlin Zhou*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对高维函数型面板中的异质结构突变检测问题，提出一种结合个体平方CUSUM能量聚合与广义功效增强分量的Energy–PE统计量，并配套基于信息准则的聚类算法估计分组数与成员，以提升在稀疏和符号异质变点下的检测功效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维函数型面板数据由跨多个个体的时间有序曲线构成，天然存在异质结构变化。当个体层面的变点信号稀疏或不同个体变化方向相反（符号异质）时，传统的截面平均型CUSUM方法会因平均效应导致信号减弱甚至相互抵消，造成显著的检验功效损失。因此需要一种能保留个体层面证据、同时对稀疏弱信号更敏感的检测方法。

Method: 作者提出Energy–PE统计量，由两部分构成：一是对每个个体的CUSUM统计量取平方后进行“能量”聚合，以在符号异质变化下保留个体层面的证据；二是一个广义功效增强（power-enhancement）分量，用于提升对稀疏弱变点信号的敏感性。在正则条件下，作者给出了该统计量的渐近性质。此外，引入潜在分组结构，并采用基于信息准则的聚类算法来估计未知的分组数量和各组组成员，以实现对异质变点的分组识别。

Result: 数值模拟结果表明，Energy–PE统计量能够控制第一类错误（size），并在稀疏和符号异质的备择情形下显著提升检验功效。日内股票数据的实证应用进一步验证了方法的有效性，并能够得到可解释的检验后分组总结。

Conclusion: Energy–PE统计量有效克服了传统截面平均CUSUM方法在稀疏信号和符号异质变化下的功效损失问题，兼顾了检验功效与事后可解释性，为高维函数型面板的异质结构突变检测提供了一种稳健且实用的工具。

Abstract: High-dimensional functional panels consist of temporally ordered curves observed across many subjects and naturally exhibit heterogeneous structural changes. Under sparse subject-level break signals or opposite-signed shifts, traditional mean-aggregated CUSUM procedures may suffer noticeable power loss due to signal attenuation or cancellation induced by cross-sectional averaging. We propose a novel Energy--PE statistic, which combines subject-wise squared CUSUM energy aggregation with a generalized power-enhancement component. The energy aggregation preserves subject-level evidence under sign-heterogeneous changes, while the power-enhancement component improves sensitivity to sparse weak break signals. Under regularity conditions, we establish the asymptotic behavior of the proposed statistic. We further incorporate a latent group structure and an information-criterion-based clustering algorithm to estimate the unknown group number and membership for heterogeneous break points. Numerical studies and an intraday stock application demonstrate that Energy--PE controls size, improves power under sparse and sign-heterogeneous alternatives, and yields interpretable post-test summaries.

</details>


### [183] [A Forecast Combination Framework for Hierarchical and Grouped Time Series Reconciliatio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13886)
*Xixi Li,Zijia Chen,James W. Taylor,Xiaojie Mao*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文将层次/分组时间序列的预测组合与预测协调两个原本独立发展的研究领域统一起来，提出一个用于预测协调的预测组合框架。其核心发现是：MinT 协调等价于对由聚合约束生成的候选预测进行 Bates–Granger 最优组合，且该最优权重问题可按底层序列分离求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测组合与预测协调两个领域长期各自独立发展，缺乏统一的理论联系。作者希望弥合这一鸿沟，为现有协调方法提供新的解释，并为设计新方法提供灵活基础。

Method: 对每个底层序列，利用聚合约束构造最大线性无关的结构化候选预测集；证明将这些候选预测组合后再聚合等价于标准无偏线性协调。在此表示下，证明均方误差最优组合权重可精确恢复 MinT 协调，并进一步证明最优权重问题可按不同底层序列分离，每个可归结为一个 Bates–Granger 最优组合。针对有限样本估计，提出模块化惩罚框架，嵌套现有 MinT 变体，并支持协方差收缩、权重惩罚和可扩展的序列级分离估计等扩展。

Result: 实验表明该框架实际可实现，与现有方法具有竞争力，并能在保持一致性的同时提升预测精度。

Conclusion: 预测组合视角一方面为现有协调方法（如 MinT）提供了新的理论解释，另一方面为灵活设计新的协调方法奠定了基础。

Abstract: Forecast combining and forecast reconciliation for hierarchical and grouped time series have largely developed as separate research areas. This paper connects the two by developing a forecast combination framework for forecast reconciliation. For each bottom-level series, we construct a maximal linearly independent set of structured candidate forecasts from aggregation constraints, and show that combining these candidates and aggregating the resulting bottom-level forecasts is equivalent to standard unbiased linear reconciliation. Within this representation, we prove that mean-squared-error optimal combination weights exactly recover the widely used Minimum Trace (MinT) reconciliation. We further show that the optimal weight problem is separable across different bottom-level series, each yielding a Bates--Granger optimal forecast combination. This reveals MinT as a collection of optimal combinations over hierarchy-induced candidate forecasts. For finite-sample estimation, we propose a modular penalized framework that nests existing MinT variants and supports rich extensions including covariance shrinkage, weight penalization, and scalable series-wise separate estimation. Empirical results show that the framework is practically implementable, competitive with existing methods, and can improve accuracy while preserving coherence. Overall, the forecast combination perspective offers new interpretations of existing reconciliation approaches and provides a flexible basis for designing new methods.

</details>


### [184] [Semi-supervised Concordance Learning for Optimal Individual Treatment Regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13945)
*Mengjiao Peng,Yong Zhou,Wenbin Lu*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在半监督数据框架下研究最优个体化治疗规则的估计：仅少量样本有结局标签，而协变量与治疗分配对所有个体可得。作者提出一种半参数方法，通过单指标核平滑进行非参数插补，再基于一致性辅助学习估计最优治疗规则，并从理论与实证两方面验证其有效性与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实应用中（如电子病历数据）由于标注成本高、时间限制与隐私顾虑，仅有极小比例样本具有结局标签，而协变量和治疗分配信息完整。传统全监督估计方法未能利用大量无标签样本，导致估计效率受限。因此需要发展能利用结局无标签样本以提升估计效率与稳健性的半监督推断方法。

Method: 提出两步半参数估计框架：第一步利用单指标核平滑（single-index kernel smoothing）对缺失结局进行灵活非参数插补，充分利用无标签样本的协变量与治疗信息；第二步基于一致性辅助学习（concordance-assisted learning）估计最优治疗规则，并建立估计量的一致性与渐近正态性。

Result: 数值模拟表明，在有限样本情形下，所提方法相比全监督估计量具有更高的估计效率与更强的稳健性；在 MIMIC-III 与 ACTG175 数据集上的应用进一步验证了该框架的实际价值。

Conclusion: 所提出的半参数半监督方法能够有效利用结局无标签样本，显著提升最优治疗规则估计的效率与稳健性，并具备良好的理论性质与实用价值。

Abstract: Finding the optimal individualized treatment rule that maps individual characteristics or contextual information to treatment assignments has been extensively investigated in existing literature, with widespread practical applications. This paper considers the estimation of optimal treatment regimes within a semi-supervised data framework (exemplified by electronic medical record data). In such settings, only a tiny proportion of observations have observed outcome labels, owing to high labeling costs, time limitations, data privacy concerns, and other constraints, while covariates and treatment assignments are available for all study subjects. We develop a semi-parametric inference method for optimal treatment regimes, which leverages outcome- unlabeled samples with complete covariate and treatment information to enhance estimation efficiency. The proposed estimation framework consists of two key steps: first, flexible nonparametric imputation via single-index kernel smoothing; second, subsequent estimation of the optimal treatment regime based on concordance-assisted learning. We establish the consistency and asymptotic normality of our proposed estimators. Numerical simulation studies demonstrate that our method achieves higher efficiency and stronger robustness relative to fully supervised estimators under finite-sample settings. We further validate the practical value of our proposed framework using the MIMIC-III and ACTG175 datasets.

</details>


### [185] [Interval Estimation of the Common Shape Parameter and Coefficient of Variation of Several Weibull Populations under Progressive Censoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13971)
*Bankitdor M. Nongrum,Adarsha Kumar Jena*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文在渐进型II型截尾下，系统比较了多个Weibull总体共同形状参数及共同变异系数的区间估计方法，并提出最优截尾方案；模拟与碳纤维强度实例表明，贝叶斯方法和方差估计恢复法表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weibull分布在可靠性等领域应用广泛，多个总体常共享形状参数（从而共享变异系数），但对截尾数据下共同形状参数的推断研究不足，尤其是区间估计缺乏系统比较。

Method: 在渐进型II型截尾下，采用四种频率学派方法（大样本理论、方差估计恢复、广义枢轴量）和贝叶斯推断构建共同形状参数及共同变异系数的区间估计，并给出最优截尾方案设计。

Result: 通过模拟研究和碳纤维强度真实数据对比分析，贝叶斯方法与方差估计恢复法构造的区间估计表现令人满意，被推荐使用。

Conclusion: 对于渐进型II型截尾下多个Weibull总体共同形状参数和共同变异系数的区间估计，贝叶斯方法和方差估计恢复方法性能优越，结合最优截尾方案可增强区间估计的稳健性。

Abstract: The Weibull distribution is one of the most flexible continuous probability distributions used to model various failure rates and skewed data in reliability engineering, industry, weather studies and cancer studies. It is a common scenario in statistical inference that several Weibull populations share the same shape parameter, which also implies that they have the same coefficient of variation. While the inferential study of the common shape parameter is often considered for complete samples, the presence of censored data requires a separate investigation that has not received enough attention in the existing literature. Therefore, the focus of this article is on a comparative study of interval estimators for the common shape parameter and the common coefficient of variation under progressive type-II censoring using frequentist methods based on large-sample theory, variance estimates recovery, generalized pivots, and Bayesian inference. An optimal censoring scheme is also proposed to enhance the robustness of interval estimation. Numerical data analyses using a simulation study and a real carbon fiber strength data example are carried out for comparison, and the results recommend the intervals based on Bayesian and variance estimates recovery methods for their satisfactory performance.

</details>


### [186] [Coherence, charity and triangulation in statistical modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13986)
*David J. T. Sumpter*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文借用戴维森“经验主义第三教条”批判贝叶斯统计学中诸多二分法（频率/贝叶斯、客观/主观概率、模型/数据、先验/后验），主张应按信念系统的内在关系来理解贝叶斯推断，并提出信念系统需满足融贯性、施惠性与三角定位三重约束，以改进统计实践。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者认为贝叶斯统计建立在若干熟悉但可能误导性的二分法之上，这些二分法可视为戴维森所批评的“图式/内容”二元论的变体。作者希望借助哲学工具揭示这些区分的局限，并重新理解信念、模型与数据的关系，从而为改进统计实践提供理论依据。

Method: 本文采用哲学批判与概念分析的方法：以“估算足球队进球频率”为例，将戴维森“第三教条”用于解构贝叶斯统计中的数对区分；主张置信度本身即客观、模型与数据应视为同一信念系统的组成部分、贝叶斯定理描述的是系统内部关系而非外部数据对模型的更新。进而援引戴维森“彻底解释”的建构性纲领，提出信念系统应满足融贯、施惠与三角定位三重约束，并论证这三者正是良好统计实践（从先验启发到模型核查）已然努力满足的要求。

Result: 作者论证了：置信度本身具有客观性；模型与数据宜视作同一信念系统的部分而非两类事物；贝叶斯定理刻画的是信念系统内部关系；真正重要的在于信念系统如何被构建、检验与重建。同时指出融贯性、施惠性与三角定位三重约束既是统计实践暗含的目标，明确讨论这些约束能改进实践。

Conclusion: 贝叶斯统计学中的传统二分法可通过戴维森框架被重新理解为信念系统内部的有机关系，而非二元对立。良好的统计实践实质上已追求融贯、施惠与三角定位三种约束，将其明朗化并加以讨论，有助于提升统计工作的质量与自我理解。

Abstract: Bayesian statistics rests on a few familiar distinctions: frequentist vs. Bayesian, objective versus subjective probability, a model versus the data it is fitted to, a prior versus a posterior. Here, I use Donald Davidson's "third dogma of empiricism" to critique such distinctions in terms of scheme/content dualisms. With a single running example -- estimating how often a football (soccer) team scores -- I argue that a degree of belief is in itself objective, that model and data are better seen as parts of one belief system than as different kinds of things, and that Bayes' theorem describes relations within that system rather than data updating a model from outside. What matters instead is how belief systems are built, checked and rebuilt. To this end, I use Davidson's constructive programme of radical interpretation to suggest that a belief system should be not only coherent (as statisticians already require) but also charitable and triangulated, answerable to other people and to a shared world. I argue that these three constraints are exactly what good statistical practice -- from eliciting priors to checking models -- already tries to satisfy and talking about them explicitly could improve this practice.

</details>


### [187] [Handling covariate shift by model averaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14025)
*Yifan Zhang,Tianfa Xie,Xinyu Zhang*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对协变量偏移下的目标域预测问题，提出一种自适应重要性加权模型平均方法，通过将密度比提高到不同指数构造候选估计量族，并以数据驱动方式加权平均，在偏差-方差间取得平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 源分布与目标分布之间的分布失配普遍存在。基于源数据拟合的模型优化的是源分布下的预测风险，对目标域可能次优。重要性加权（密度比校正）是自然解法，但少数过大的密度比值会在有限样本中显著放大估计量的方差，需要权衡偏差与方差。

Method: 将重要性加权修正的程度视为模型不确定性来源：通过对估计的密度比取一系列指数（端点分别为普通最小二乘与标准重要性加权最小二乘）构造一族自适应重要性加权最小二乘估计量，再对其形成数据驱动的凸组合（模型平均）。在模型误设下证明该平均估计量相对于不可行的最佳凸组合具有渐近最优性；在正确设定下，通过发散惩罚使所选权重集中于普通最小二乘端点。

Result: 在模型误设下，所提模型平均估计量相对不可行最优凸组合渐近最优；在正确设定下，发散惩罚使权重聚集于普通最小二乘端点。模拟与真实数据应用显示该方法在所考察情形下取得有竞争力的目标域预测表现。

Conclusion: 该自适应重要性加权模型平均方法有效平衡了协变量偏移校正中的偏差-方差权衡，兼具理论保证与实用预测性能，是处理目标域预测的有效工具。

Abstract: Distributional mismatch between the data used to construct a statistical procedure and the population to which it is ultimately applied is pervasive in modern data analysis. We study covariate shift, a fundamental instance of this problem, and develop an adaptive importance-weighted model averaging method for prediction when labeled observations are available from a source distribution, whereas only unlabeled covariates are observed from the target distribution. Procedures fitted directly to the source sample generally optimize prediction risk under the source distribution and may therefore be suboptimal for target prediction. Importance weighting by the density ratio between the target and source covariate marginals provides a natural correction, but a small number of large density-ratio values can substantially inflate the variance of the resulting estimator in finite samples. We address this bias-variance trade-off by treating the degree of importance-weighting correction as a source of model uncertainty. Specifically, we construct a family of adaptive importance-weighted least-squares estimators by raising the estimated density ratio to a range of exponents, with the endpoints corresponding to ordinary least squares and standard importance-weighted least squares, and form a data-driven average over these candidates. Under model misspecification, the proposed model averaging estimator is shown to be asymptotically optimal relative to the infeasible best convex combination of the candidate estimators. Under correct specification, a diverging penalty is shown to make the selected weights concentrate near the ordinary least-squares endpoint. Simulations and a real-data application show that the proposed method achieves competitive target-prediction performance across the settings considered.

</details>


### [188] [A Unified Bayesian Model for Voter Turnout Estimation: Combining Surveys, Aggregate Data, and Selection Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14062)
*Margus Niitsoo,Reimo Rebane,Tarmo Jüristo*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一个贝叶斯层次框架，联合集成个体调查数据、官方总体投票率边际和人口普查单元计数，以改进人口子群体的选举投票率小区域估计。框架包含三个模型：多水平生态推断（EI）、Poll-and-Margin（PM）和全选择（FS）模型，其中FS通过Heckman式选择校正和信息先验应对非随机无响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确估计人口子群体的投票率对政治分析至关重要，但方法上困难重重：调查数据存在过度报告、非代表性和非可忽略无响应问题，而基于聚合数据的生态推断易受生态谬误影响。作者旨在构建一个能联合利用个体调查数据与官方聚合边际的框架，以克服单一数据源各自的缺陷。

Method: 提出贝叶斯层次框架，联合个体调查数据、官方总体投票率边际和人口普查单元计数。包含三个模型：(1) 多水平生态推断（EI）模型，将MRP式结构扩展到聚合数据；(2) Poll-and-Margin（PM）模型，在统一似然中联合拟合调查数据和边际；(3) 全选择（FS）模型，在随机接触假设下加入Heckman式选择校正，并使用信息先验以保证可识别性。

Result: 在基于经验人口普查人口统计的受控选择机制模拟中，FS相比既有基准有显著改进，尤其在强选择偏差或重度删失情况下表现突出，但对未建模的选择噪声敏感。在2023年爱沙尼亚议会选举的应用中，PM在可用验证边际上的表现与当前最先进方法相近，FS有适度改进，但该改进依赖信息先验。

Conclusion: 该贝叶斯层次框架通过联合调查数据、官方边际和普查计数，有效提升人口子群体投票率的小区域估计。FS模型在强选择偏差下尤其有效，但其性能受未建模选择噪声影响，且应用中的改进依赖信息先验的选择。

Abstract: Accurate small-area estimation of voter turnout for demographic subgroups is crucial for political analysis but methodologically challenging. Survey data suffer from over-reporting, non-representativeness, and non-ignorable non-response, while ecological inference (EI) from aggregate data is vulnerable to the ecological fallacy. We propose a Bayesian hierarchical framework that jointly integrates individual-level survey data, official aggregate turnout margins, and census cell counts. The framework comprises three models: a multilevel ecological inference (EI) model that extends MRP-style structure to aggregate data, a Poll-and-Margin (PM) model that jointly fits survey and margins in one coherent likelihood, and a Full Selection (FS) model that adds a Heckman-style selection correction under a random-contact assumption and uses informative priors for identification. In simulations with controlled selection mechanisms based on empirical census demographics, FS substantially improves over established benchmarks, particularly under strong selection bias or heavy censoring, but is sensitive to unmodeled selection noise. In an application to the 2023 Estonian parliamentary election, PM performs similarly to the current state of the art on available validation margins, with FS showing a modest improvement that depends on the informative priors.

</details>


### [189] [Generation-Powered Inference for Distribution-Valued Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14542)
*Yijiao Zhang,Hongzhe Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出"生成驱动推断"（GPI）框架，利用辅助生成模型的分布值预测来改进对分布值参数（如 Wasserstein 重心）的统计推断，并通过函数值桥表示将非线性 Wasserstein 空间中的推断转化为 Hilbert 空间中的均值函数估计，在理论性质、模拟和单细胞 Perturb-seq 数据上验证了其有效性与稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代生成模型（如单细胞基因组学中预测细胞对遗传扰动的响应）越来越多地输出分布值结果，这些模型能提供有价值的辅助信息，但本身并不完美，因此需要一种统计方法，既能利用生成模型的预测，又不依赖其正确性。

Method: 提出生成驱动推断（GPI）通用框架；聚焦 Wasserstein 重心及相关分布泛函，引入函数值桥表示（function-valued bridge representation），将非线性 Wasserstein 空间中的推断转化为 Hilbert 空间中均值函数的估计，从而建立类似预测驱动推断（prediction-powered inference）的增强估计框架；构建一族具有最优信息借用（optimal information borrowing）的 GPI 估计量，并推导一致性、渐近正态性与同时置信带，以及线性泛函与 Wasserstein 距离的有效推断。

Result: 模拟研究表明，相比仅使用标注数据的方法，GPI 估计量具有效率增益，且在生成模型设定错误（misspecification）下仍表现稳健。在 K562 细胞的 Perturb-seq 研究中，利用 State 基础模型生成的合成扰动响应，改进了与 40S 核糖体模块扰动相关的通路级共识基因表达分布的推断。

Conclusion: GPI 为在生成模型预测不完美的情况下利用其辅助信息改进分布值参数的统计推断提供了一套理论完善、方法通用且在实际生物数据上有效的框架。

Abstract: Modern generative models increasingly produce distribution-valued outputs, such as predicted cellular responses to genetic perturbations in single-cell genomics. While these models provide valuable auxiliary information, they are inherently imperfect, creating a need for statistical methods that leverage their predictions without relying on their correctness. We propose generation-powered inference (GPI), a general framework for improving inference on distribution-valued parameters using auxiliary generative models. Focusing on Wasserstein barycenters and related distributional functionals, we introduce a function-valued bridge representation that transforms inference in the nonlinear Wasserstein space into estimation of a mean function in a Hilbert space, enabling an augmented estimation framework analogous to prediction-powered inference. We develop a family of GPI estimators with optimal information borrowing, establish consistency, asymptotic normality, and simultaneous confidence bands, and derive valid inference for linear functionals and Wasserstein distances. Simulation studies demonstrate efficiency gains over labeled-data-only methods and robust performance under generative model misspecification. We illustrate the proposed framework using a Perturb-seq study of K562 cells, where synthetic perturbation responses generated by the State foundation model are used to improve inference for pathway-level consensus gene expression distributions associated with perturbations of the 40S ribosome module.

</details>


### [190] [An integration of decision trees into latent class modeling with covariates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14091)
*Johan Lyrvall,Felix Clouth*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将决策树拟合到潜在类别的全新方法，用可解释的树结构代替逻辑回归来刻画协变量对潜在类别成员的影响，重点针对二值协变量，并提出了两种剪枝策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统潜类分析（LCA）文献主要依赖逻辑模型刻画协变量与类别成员的关系，但在协变量间存在复杂交互作用时，逻辑模型容易因遗漏交互项而产生错误设定，且纳入高阶交互项会急剧增加解释难度。因此需要一种能自然捕捉交互作用且易于解释的建模工具。

Method: 将决策树建模直接整合进潜类分析框架，用树结构刻画协变量效应，引导读者沿预测变量组合路径得到最终的条件分类；基于成熟的模型估计流程，可方便地在现有软件中实现。本文聚焦二值协变量，并提出两种决策树剪枝策略，辅以真实数据实例说明。

Result: 所提方法不需要对带协变量的潜类模型引入非传统假设，易于解释且可直接在现有软件中实现；文中展示了该方法及两种剪枝策略在真实数据上的应用效果。

Conclusion: 本研究旨在潜类分析方法学文献中开辟一条新的研究方向，并通过提供新颖的数据分析工具推动应用研究进展。未来工作包括将该方法推广到多分类与连续协变量（开发更先进的预测变量内部分裂程序），以及发展和评估更多备选剪枝策略。

Abstract: We propose a novel methodology for fitting decision trees to latent classes. The latent class analysis methodological literature has previously been focusing on logistic models of class membership given covariates, which has important drawbacks in the presence of complex interactions between covariates: logistic models are easily misspecified by omitting some interaction terms and complexity of interpretation increases rapidly with inclusion of higher-order interaction terms. Our proposed approach directly integrates decision tree modeling into the latent class analysis framework to model the covariate effects as an easily interpretable tree leading the eye through combinations of predictors to a final conditional classification. The novel methodology does not require any non-traditional assumptions for latent class models with covariates, is based on well-established routines of model estimation, and can be readily implemented in existing software. In the present paper, we focus on decision trees for binary covariates. We present the proposed approach, describe two tree pruning strategies, and provide a real-data illustration. Important extensions include generalizing the approach to multinomial and continuous covariates by developing more advanced within-predictor splitting procedures, and developing and evaluating alternative tree pruning strategies. The ultimate aim of this work is to initiate a novel research line in the latent class analysis methodological literature, and to facilitate the advancement of applied research via a novel data analytical tool.

</details>
