### [191] [FastJM: An R Package for Efficient Implementation of Semiparametric Joint Models for Longitudinal and Survival Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14127)
*Shanpeng Li,Emily Ouyang,Ace Isabel Mejia-Sanchez,Xinping Cui,Gang Li*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文介绍R包FastJM，用于高效拟合三类半参数联合模型，通过定制线性扫描算法在EM框架内快速更新非参数基线风险，并提供建模、推断、动态预测及性能评估的统一接口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联合模型能灵活刻画纵向过程与时间-事件过程之间的关联，在生物医学研究中应用广泛；但对大规模、复杂的生物医学数据而言，拟合联合模型计算开销大，存在显著的计算瓶颈，亟需高效的计算工具。

Method: 提出R包FastJM，在期望最大化(EM)框架下采用定制线性扫描算法高效更新非参数基线风险，解决半参数联合建模的主要计算瓶颈；支持三类半参数联合模型（单一纵向生物标志物、多个纵向生物标志物、以及具有异质性个体内变异的单纵向标志物模型），并通过与里程碑多元联合建模框架集成来支持常用的时变潜在关联结构。

Result: FastJM提供统一的模型设定、估计、推断、可视化、动态预测及预测性能评估接口，包括交叉验证的时间依赖性准确性度量和时间无关的一致性统计量；通过可复现示例展示了其核心功能。

Conclusion: FastJM为大规模复杂生物医学数据下的半参数联合建模提供了计算高效的频繁学派估计工具，显著缓解了非参数基线风险更新带来的计算瓶颈，并具备完整的建模与预测评估功能。

Abstract: Joint models provide a flexible framework for characterizing the association between longitudinal and time-to-event processes and have been widely applied in biomedical research. However, fitting joint models can be computationally challenging for large-scale and complex biomedical data. This paper introduces the \proglang{R} package \pkg{FastJM}, which provides computationally efficient frequentist estimation for three classes of semiparametric joint models: joint models with a single longitudinal biomarker, joint models with multiple longitudinal biomarkers, and joint models with a single longitudinal biomarker with heterogeneous within-subject (WS) variability. Within an expectation--maximization framework, \pkg{FastJM} employs customized linear-scan algorithms to efficiently update the nonparametric baseline hazards, thereby addressing a major computational bottleneck in semiparametric joint modeling. The package also supports commonly used time-dependent latent association structures by integrating these algorithms with a landmark multivariate joint modeling framework. \pkg{FastJM} provides a unified interface for model specification, estimation, inference, visualization, dynamic prediction, and prediction performance assessment, including cross-validated time-dependent accuracy measures and time-independent concordance statistics. We describe the underlying methodology and software implementation and demonstrate the main functionality of \pkg{FastJM} through reproducible examples.

</details>


### [192] [Exact Likelihood Inference for Snowball-Sampled Erdős-Rényi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14129)
*Nurzhan Sapargali,Sergio Buttazzo,G\''oran Kauermann*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 针对链接追踪（如滚雪球抽样）所得网络数据的抽样机制与网络结构相互依赖导致的推断偏差，本文给出了在 Erdős–Rényi 模型下 r 波滚雪球样本（全邻域招募）的精确似然解，并提出无偏的最大似然估计量与有效置信区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过滚雪球等链接追踪设计采集的网络数据，其抽样机制依赖于待估计的网络结构本身；若忽略这种依赖、把观测样本当作完整网络处理，会导致严重有偏的推断。该选择问题一般难以处理，需在可解设定下给出精确修正。

Method: 针对来自 Erdős–Rényi 总体的 r 波滚雪球样本（全邻域招募），推导样本的精确似然函数，证明其构成关于边概率 π 的弯曲指数族，并具有低维充分统计量；基于此构造正确考虑抽样设计的最大似然估计量（充分利用极小充分统计量所含信息）。置信区间则通过对基于精确抽样分布的检验求逆得到，抽样分布用蒙特卡洛模拟近似。

Result: 模拟研究表明，相比朴素估计量，所提修正显著降低偏差，即使在样本仅覆盖网络约 0.1% 时仍基本无偏；构造的置信区间在多种边概率和波数设定下，覆盖率在蒙特卡洛误差范围内达到名义水平。

Conclusion: 在 Erdős–Rényi 模型与全邻域 r 波滚雪球设计下，抽样选择问题可获得精确解；由此得到的 MLE 能有效消除链接追踪抽样导致的偏差，且所建置信区间具有正确的覆盖率，为基于此类网络样本的统计推断提供了可靠工具。

Abstract: Network data obtained through link-tracing designs, such as snowball sampling, are collected through a mechanism that depends on the very structure the analysis seeks to estimate. Ignoring this dependence and treating the observed sample as though it were itself a complete network can lead to substantially biased inference. While the resulting selection problem is intractable in general, we show that it admits an exact solution for $r$-wave snowball samples, with full-neighbourhood recruitment, drawn from an Erdős--Rényi population. We derive the exact likelihood of such a sample and show that it defines a curved exponential family in the edge probability $π$, with a low-dimensional sufficient statistic. Building on this result, we obtain the maximum likelihood estimator of $π$ that correctly accounts for the sampling design and, as a function of the minimal sufficient statistic, makes full use of the information in the sample. Simulation studies show that this correction substantially reduces bias relative to the naive estimator, remaining effectively unbiased even when the sample covers as little as 0.1\% of the network. We further construct valid confidence intervals for $π$ by inverting a test built on the exact sampling distribution, approximated via Monte Carlo simulation. Simulation studies confirm that these confidence intervals attain the nominal coverage level within Monte Carlo error across a range of edge probabilities and numbers of waves.

</details>


### [193] [Change Point Detection and Localization in High-Dimensional Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14344)
*Patrick Bastian,Daria Tieplova,Nina Dörnemann,Tim Kutta*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文提出针对高维时间序列变点检测的新推断工具，涵盖顺序（在线）检测与回顾性多变点定位两类问题，并引入高维情形下的Hölder-Gauss近似理论作为统计验证基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维时间序列中变点可能稀疏且在不同坐标上异步出现，传统基于固定维度的Hölder不变性原理无法直接扩展到高维，需要新的统计工具来检测稀疏、异步的变点并给出全局受控的置信区间。

Method: 基于最大范数构造检验统计量，以增强对稀疏异步变点的功效；采用多尺度统计量在不同分辨率下搜索变点；提出高维版本的Hölder-Gauss近似，将其解释为高维部分和过程的Hölder范数的高斯近似，用于验证统计方法。

Result: 通过模拟研究评估了方法的有限样本表现，并将方法应用于加州山火导致的空气污染数据，其中污染在不同监测站之间异步发生，验证了方法在实际高维面板数据中的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的多尺度最大范数统计量与Hölder-Gauss近似为高维时间序列的顺序变点检验和回顾性变点定位提供了有效的推断工具，且Hölder-Gauss近似具有超越变点检测的广泛应用前景（如高维平稳性检验）。

Abstract: We present new inference tools for change point detection in high-dimensional time series. We discuss two distinct statistical applications: First, sequential change point testing in an incoming data-stream. Second, retrospective localization of multiple changes, with confidence intervals at a globally controlled error level. Test statistics are built on the maximum norm to generate power against sparse and asynchronous changes. Both problems are tackled by related multiscale statistics that search for changes in the data at many different levels of resolution. For fixed dimension, our statistical approaches can be validated using traditional Hölderian invariance principles. In this paper, we present the high-dimensional analogue: Hölder-Gauss-approximations, which can be (roughly) interpreted as the Gaussian approximation for a Hölder-norm of the high-dimensional partial sum process. Such approximations are of interest beyond change point detection and can be used for other problems such as for stationarity testing in high dimensions.
  We evaluate finite-sample performance in a simulation study and give an application to air contamination due to wildfires in California, which occurs asynchronously across a panel of measuring stations.

</details>


### [194] [Joint Estimation of Sparse Multilayer Networks via Graph Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14536)
*Youngseok Song,Sofia C. Olhede*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 本文针对同一组个体上存在多种交互类型的多重网络数据，提出基于图极限（缩放图元组）的非参数联合估计方法——多重网络直方图（multi-network histogram），通过块模型近似刻画各层不同的稀疏性与连接结构，并利用共享潜变量实现层间信息共享，从而提升估计精度、缩小最优带宽，尤其在稀疏层中实现高分辨率估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代应用中网络数据常表现为同一组顶点上的多重交互。由于顶点共享，各层结构可以相互辅助解释，联合建模能够提升对各层交互生成机制的刻画能力；同时需要处理各层稀疏性不同、结构异质等问题，而单层独立建模难以充分利用层间信息。

Method: 采用图极限框架，将多重网络建模为一组缩放图元（scaled set of graphons），并引入跨层共享的潜变量以刻画异质性。在此基础上提出非参数联合估计器——多重网络直方图，基于块模型（blockmodel）近似对各层进行估计；进一步推导了该估计的加权均方积分误差上界，并据此求解使误差最小化的最优带宽。

Result: 建立了多重网络直方图的理论性质，给出加权均方积分误差的上界并推导出最优带宽；证明通过跨层信息共享，联合建模能降低估计误差并得到更小的最优带宽，从而即使在被测层较为稀疏时也能实现高分辨率估计。模拟研究与印度村庄社会经济网络的实证应用验证了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 基于图极限的缩放图元组建模与多重网络直方图提供了一种统一且理论上可证的非参数联合估计框架，能够有效利用层间共享信息、适应各层不同稀疏性与结构，降低估计误差并提升稀疏层的分辨率，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Network datasets in modern applications often involve multiple types of interactions occurring over a shared set of individuals. Characterizing the generating mechanisms of these interactions can be enhanced by joint modelling, as shared vertices allow layers to help explain the structure of other layers. We model multiplex observations using graph limits, called a scaled set of graphons, and develop a nonparametric joint estimator based on blockmodel approximations, termed the multi-network histogram. This nonparametric framework captures each layer's varying sparsity and connection structure, accounting for heterogeneity via shared latent variables across all layers. We establish the theoretical properties of the multi-network histogram, providing an upper bound for the weighted mean integrated squared error and deriving the optimal bandwidth that minimizes this error. By leveraging information across layers, this joint modelling achieves a reduction in error and a smaller optimal bandwidth, which enables high-resolution estimation even in sparser layers. Its usefulness is demonstrated through simulation studies and an application to socioeconomic networks in an Indian village.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [195] [Inducing Reward-Free Judging Rubrics that Reduce Over-Crediting in Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13564)
*Darragh Quinn,David Dylan,Roisin Healy,Fionn Carroll,Maeve Donnelly,Cormac Sheehan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 RubricForge，通过反思式进化从少量真值标注轨迹中自动生成可读的评测准则文本，作为无环境奖励场景下可信度高、可归因的自动评判器，显著降低“误判失败轨迹为成功”的假通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模评估语言模型智能体越来越依赖另一个语言模型作为自动评判器，因为在部署时环境奖励（金标准）昂贵、缓慢或不可用。现有评判器要么手工编写评分标准（如 G-Eval），要么微调判断模型权重，二者都倾向于把“输出流畅但实际失败”的轨迹误判为成功。作者认为，对于无奖励的评测器，假通过率（把坏智能体误判为好而直接上线）才是部署真正关心的指标，而非笼统的一致率，因此需要一个以真实结果为依据、可信任的评判器。

Method: RubricForge 从小量带真值标签的轨迹中归纳出评测准则文本，并通过“反思式进化”（reflective evolution）针对已标注轨迹迭代优化该准则，以最大化与环境奖励的一致性；随后冻结该准则，并以单次模型调用（无需环境访问）对留出轨迹进行评判。由于最终产物是人类可读文本，每个判定都能追溯到具体命名的准则。

Result: 使用同一个冻结的 7B 模型同时作为智能体和评判器，在 tau-bench（220 次 rollout 中取 173 条标注轨迹）和 WebShop（160 条）上评测。主要收益体现在忠实度（faithfulness）而非原始一致率：与通用 G-Eval 评判器相比，总体一致率提升不显著（McNemar p = 0.248），绝对分数校准方面通用评判器还略占优势（|err| 差值 -0.048，p = 2×10⁻⁴）。但 RubricForge 将失败轨迹的误判率降低约一半（tau-bench 假通过率 0.115 vs 0.173，含 3 次过度信任的捕捉且零逆转），并对 WebShop 分级结果的排序更忠实（Spearman 0.410 vs 0.370）。

Conclusion: 作者结论是：对无奖励的评测器而言，假通过率而非总体一致率才是部署相关的核心指标，因为假通过会把损坏的智能体直接上线，而假失败最多只损失一次重试成本。RubricForge 通过将文本化、可归因的准则与真实结果对齐，在控制假通过率方面取得关键改进，为无环境奖励下的智能体评测提供了更可信的方案。

Abstract: Evaluating language-model agents at scale increasingly relies on a second language model as an automatic judge, because the gold signal, an executable environment reward, is expensive, slow, or unavailable at deployment time. Such a judge is a reward-free proxy whose value depends on whether it can be trusted, yet existing judges either hand-write the scoring rubric, as in G-Eval, or fine-tune the judge's weights, and both tend to credit fluent but unsuccessful trajectories as successes. We instead induce the text of an agent-judging rubric from a small set of ground-truth-labeled trajectories, grounding it in true outcomes. We present RubricForge, which evolves a judge rubric by reflective evolution against labeled trajectories to maximize agreement with the environment reward, freezes it, and applies it to held-out trajectories in one model call with no environment access. The optimized artifact is human-readable text, so every verdict is attributable to named criteria. Using one frozen 7B model as both agent and judge, on tau-bench (173 labeled trajectories drawn from 220 rollouts) and WebShop (160), the principal gain is faithfulness rather than raw agreement. The edge over a generic G-Eval judge is not statistically significant (McNemar p = 0.248), and absolute-score calibration marginally favors the generic judge (|err| difference -0.048, p = 2x10^-4). Yet RubricForge over-credits failed trajectories roughly half as often (0.115 vs. 0.173 false-pass rate on tau-bench, with three over-credit catches and zero reversals) and ranks graded WebShop outcomes more faithfully (Spearman 0.410 vs. 0.370). For a reward-free evaluator the false-pass rate, not aggregate agreement, is the deployment-relevant quantity, since a false pass ships a broken agent whereas a false fail merely costs a retry.

</details>


### [196] [Depth-Aware Sensitivity Analysis of Mixture-of-Experts Models via Magnitude-Based Expert Masking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13565)
*Pradeep Kumar Sharma,Shantanu Godbole,Hritvik Shrivastava*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B（40 层 MoE、每层 256 专家、top-8 路由）进行分层敏感度分析，发现 MoE 层对专家掩码的敏感度与深度强相关：早期与中层脆弱，晚期（尤其 35-39 层）可容忍激进的低幅值专家掩码，从而在不显著损失质量的前提下大幅压缩专家数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管 MoE 架构已广泛用于扩展 LLM 并保持计算效率，但单个 MoE 层的相对重要性，特别是针对模型压缩的层重要性，仍缺乏系统刻画。作者希望为深度感知的 MoE 专家掩码与压缩提供实证基础。

Method: 在 XLCoST 跨语言代码翻译基准上，采用基于幅值的专家掩码对 Qwen3.6-35B-A3B 进行系统分层敏感度分析；开展多阶段研究，覆盖 100、300、500 个 prompt 的评估规模，在三台 H100 GPU 服务器上运行。比较全局平坦掩码与晚期聚焦掩码策略，并进一步考察 top-k 路由宽度从 8 降到 6（每 token 活跃专家数）的效果。

Result: 层敏感度呈强深度依赖性：第 0-9 层和第 10-29 层对专家掩码高度脆弱，而第 30-39 层、尤其 35-39 层可容忍激进的低幅值专家掩码。在 300-prompt 规模下，30% 的全局平坦掩码仅保留 150/300 个 Good+Similar 输出，而晚期聚焦策略在掩码 640-1,145 个专家的同时保留 249-255/300 个。在 500-prompt 留出验证切片上，窄域超晚期策略（35-39 层 @ 50%）实现最佳质量/掩码专家权衡，保留 419/500 个 Good+Similar 输出，仅掩码 10,240 个专家中的 640 个。top-k 从 8 降到 6 在 100-prompt 探查上带来显著实际延迟下降且无 Good+Similar 损失，但与激进专家掩码尚不能干净地复合。

Conclusion: 研究为深度感知的 MoE 专家掩码奠定了实证基础，并指出了通向物理权重手术、基于激活的专家评分以及基于训练的恢复的可行路径。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures scale large language models (LLMs) while preserving computational efficiency through sparse activation. Despite their widespread adoption, the relative importance of individual MoE layers remains insufficiently characterized, particularly for model compression. This paper presents a systematic layer-wise sensitivity analysis of the Qwen3.6-35B-A3B model (40 MoE layers, 256 experts per layer, top-8 routing) using magnitude-based expert masking on the XLCoST cross-lingual code translation benchmark. We conduct a multi-phase study spanning 100, 300, and 500 prompt evaluation scales across three H100 GPU servers. Our central finding is that layer sensitivity is strongly depth-dependent: early layers (0-9) and middle layers (10-29) are highly fragile to expert masking, while late layers (30-39), and especially very-late layers (35-39), tolerate aggressive masking of low-magnitude experts. Flat all-layer masking at 30% retains only 150/300 Good+Similar outputs at 300-prompt scale, whereas late-focused policies retain 249-255/300 while masking 640-1,145 experts. On a later 500-prompt held-out validation slice, the narrow very-late policy (layers 35-39 @ 50%) achieves the strongest quality/masked-expert tradeoff among tested candidates, retaining 419/500 Good+Similar outputs while masking only 640 of 10,240 total experts. We additionally characterize top-k routing width reduction from 8 to 6 active experts per token, which shows a large observed wall-clock reduction on a 100-prompt probe with no Good+Similar loss, though it does not yet compose cleanly with aggressive expert masking. These findings provide an empirical foundation for depth-aware MoE expert masking and establish a practical path toward physical weight surgery, activation-based expert scoring, and training-based recovery.

</details>


### [197] [Modular Cognitive Architecture Emerges in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13567)
*Pengrui Han,Jacob Andreas,Evelina Fedorenko,Andrea Gregor de Varda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过分析46个认知任务（语言、形式推理、社会推理、物理推理）在大语言模型中的电路结构，发现LLM自发形成了与人脑相似的模块化组织——同类任务共享神经元、异类任务使用不同神经元，表明模块化可能是智能系统的基本属性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人脑表现出高度的功能特化，不同网络分别支持语言、形式推理、他人心理推理和物理世界推理。作者希望探究这种模块化组织究竟是智能系统构建的基本原理，还是仅属于生物大脑的进化偶然产物。

Method: 对N=46个覆盖四大认知领域的任务进行电路分析（circuit analyses），考察各任务所激活（招募）的神经元重叠情况，并将LLM的模块化结构与人类大脑的功能网络进行对照。

Result: LLM形成了与人脑镜像的模块化架构：在人脑中依赖同一网络的任务在LLM中招募重叠的神经元，而依赖不同网络的任务则招募不同的神经元。

Conclusion: 大脑与神经网络中模块化的趋同涌现表明，模块化可能是智能系统的一种基本属性，而非生物进化独有的偶然结果。

Abstract: The human brain exhibits a striking degree of functional specialization, with distinct networks supporting language, formal reasoning, reasoning about other minds, and reasoning about the physical world. Is this modular organization a fundamental principle of how intelligent systems must be built, or an evolutionary accident specific to biological brains? Here, we test whether a similar organization emerges in Large Language Models--another class of intelligent systems created through a very different optimization process. Using circuit analyses across N=46 tasks spanning four cognitive domains (language, formal reasoning, social reasoning, physical reasoning), we find that LLMs develop a modular architecture that mirrors the human brain: tasks drawing on the same network in humans recruit overlapping neurons in LLMs, whereas tasks drawing on different networks recruit distinct neurons. The convergent emergence of modularity in brains and neural networks suggests that it may be a fundamental property of intelligent systems.

</details>


### [198] [A Year in LLM Serving: Workload Evolution, Caching and Load-Balancing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13573)
*William Nixon,Jon Durbin,Florian Standhartinger,Haryadi S. Gunawi,Juncheng Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对来自 Chutes 平台、长达一年的生产级 LLM 服务负载轨迹进行了全局刻画与纵向研究，揭示了工作负载随时间的演化及用户-模型交互结构，并承诺公开完整轨迹以支持未来研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 服务已成为关键云负载，但现有研究在规模与范围上有限，观察时间短、对用户与模型交互的可见性不足，无法完整刻画负载随时间演化的规律及其如何塑造生产流量。

Method: 基于 Chutes 平台一年的生产轨迹，从聚合、时间、模型级和用户级四个视角进行全局表征与纵向分析，覆盖热门与长尾模型及众多用户，并公开完整数据。

Result: 揭示了隐藏在聚合视图背后的工作负载演化规律与用户-模型结构，包括多种模型与用户交互模式，为理解和基准测试 LLM 服务系统提供了更完整的依据。

Conclusion: 通过发布完整一年的生产轨迹，本文弥补了现有研究的规模与范围不足，使下游研究能够基于真实生产行为而非抽样或合成负载开展，推动 LLM 服务系统研究与基准测试的发展。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) serving has become a critical cloud workload, and realistic traces are essential for motivating and benchmarking serving systems. However, existing LLM serving workload studies remain limited in scale and scope. They often observe short time periods and provide limited visibility into how users interact with models in production. As a result, they do not fully capture how LLM serving workloads evolve over time or how user-model interactions shape production traffic.
  In this work, we further the understanding of real-world LLM serving workloads through both a global characterization and a longitudinal study of a one-year production trace from Chutes. Unlike prior studies, our trace captures full production behavior across many models and users, including both popular and long-tail models. We analyze the workload from aggregate, temporal, model-level, and user-level perspectives, revealing workload evolution and user-model structure that are typically hidden behind aggregate views. To support future research, we will release the full one-year trace with the paper, enabling downstream studies of production behavior without relying on sampled or synthetically generated workloads.

</details>


### [199] [Agentao: A Governed Local-First Runtime for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13574)
*Bo Jin,Qiang Jiao,Xin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Agentao 是一个受治理的本地优先运行时，用于工具型 LLM Agent。它通过分层架构将模型生成的动作提案与主机授权的执行相分离，并引入权限系统、执行追踪与协议边界等运行时抽象，使 Agent 在行为上更可治理、可审计，适合主机控制的本地环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM Agent 日益成为调用工具、修改本地状态、使用持久化内存并交互外部协议的执行系统。这些能力带来过度授权、审计性弱、提示注入、工具投毒以及不受控副作用等风险，因此需要一种可治理、可检查、由主机控制的执行运行时。

Method: 提出 Agentao，一种受治理的本地优先运行时。其核心是分层架构，将模型生成的动作提案与主机授权执行分离，包含：主机面接口、主机契约、运行时核心、经权限中介的工具系统，以及内存、回放、插件、技能、子 Agent 和协议集成等支撑子系统。文章描述了系统的动机、威胁模型、设计目标、治理模型、执行流水线和结构化事件接口。

Result: Agentao 未提供形式化安全保证，而是演示了如何将权限、状态、协议边界与执行追踪显式地作为运行时抽象。代码已公开于 https://github.com/jin-bo/agentao。

Conclusion: Agentao 展示了通过把权限、状态、协议边界和执行追踪做成显式运行时抽象，可以构建更可治理、可检查且适合主机控制本地环境的 Agent 系统；它是架构与治理设计上的实践验证而非形式化安全方案。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly operate as execution systems that invoke tools, modify local state, use persistent memory, and interact with external protocols. These capabilities make agents useful, but they also introduce risks related to over-privileged actions, weak auditability, prompt injection, tool poisoning, and uncontrolled side effects. This paper presents Agentao, a governed local-first runtime for tool-using LLM agents. Agentao separates model-generated action proposals from host-authorized execution through a layered architecture consisting of host-facing surfaces, a host contract, a runtime core, a permission-mediated tool system, and supporting subsystems for memory, replay, plugins, skills, sub-agents, and protocol integration. We describe the motivation, threat model, design goals, governance model, execution pipeline, and structured event interface of the system. Agentao does not provide formal safety guarantees; rather, it demonstrates how permissions, state, protocol boundaries, and execution traces can be made explicit runtime abstractions for building agents that are more governable, inspectable, and suitable for host-controlled local environments. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/jin-bo/agentao.

</details>


### [200] [AI Evaluation Should Work With Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13577)
*Jan Kulveit,Gavin Leech,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Raymond Douglas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文主张将AI评估范式从追求超越人类的自主性能转向评估人机团队的协作表现，以促进AI成为人类能力的补充而非替代。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的AI评估范式聚焦于超人类的自主性能，其隐含目标是取代人类，作者认为这一方向正在误导AI发展；为引导AI成为人类能力的真正补充并带来更好的社会结果，有必要改变评估范式。

Method: 本文是一篇立场/观点论文（position paper），采用论证性论述方法，提出以"人机团队表现"作为新的AI评估核心标准，替代以"超人类自主性能"为核心的现行范式，并阐述这一协作式转向的理由与预期影响。

Result: 作者论证了协作式评估转向能够培育出真正补充人类能力的AI系统，从而比当前以取代人类为目标的评估流程带来显著更优的社会结果。

Conclusion: AI社区应放弃以超人类自主性能为目标的评估范式，转而评估人机协作团队的表现，以实现更符合社会利益的AI发展路径。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the dominant paradigm of AI evaluation (which focuses on superhuman autonomous performance and so implicitly targets the goal of replacing humans) is guiding AI development in the wrong direction. Instead, the AI community should pivot to evaluating the performance of human--AI teams. We argue that this collaborative shift will foster AI systems that act as true complements to human capabilities and therefore lead to far better societal outcomes than will the current process.

</details>


### [201] [Stable Miscalibration in Large Language Models: A Practical View of High-Confidence Errors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13591)
*Akira Okutomi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型中“高置信错误”的一种新解释——稳定误校准：错误答案在小扰动下保持局部稳定，而非单纯由推理脆弱导致。作者结合输出层审计分数与内部敏感性探针，发现自我批判提示能一致降低隐状态敏感性（局部稳定化），但并未改善校准，高置信错误未必比高置信正确更敏感，说明部分错误是“稳定且误校准”而非“脆弱”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常把高置信错误归因于内部推理的脆弱性，作者质疑这一主流假设，提出并检验另一种可能：某些高置信错误可能并非脆弱，而是在局部扰动下保持稳定的“稳定误校准”，从而引导更准确的诊断与干预方向。

Method: 作者组合两类诊断手段：(1) 标签感知的输出层审计分数，在强制作答基线（forced-answer baseline）下按置信度变化与过度自信错误对领域进行排序；(2) 内部敏感性探针，测量隐状态（hidden-state）的移动。在多领域二元事实审计集上验证审计分数与弃权式自我批判（abstention-aware self-critique）降低决策损失的关系，并在三个开源权重模型上分析自我批判提示对各层隐状态敏感性的影响。

Result: 输出层审计分数能追踪弃权式自我批判降低决策损失的位置，但直接的有标签基线对同一增益的排序更强；自我批判提示在三个开源模型中一致地降低各层隐状态敏感性，支持“提示诱导的局部稳定化”而非纯粹的输出层弃权模式；然而审计定义的高置信错误并不比高置信正确答案更局部敏感，说明部分高置信错误是稳定且误校准的，而非仅仅脆弱。

Conclusion: 自我批判提示带来了局部的内部稳定化，但这并不等同于校准改善：高置信错误可能既稳定又误校准。因此，不能简单将高置信错误诊断为推理脆弱，需要区分“脆弱性错误”与“稳定误校准错误”以设计针对性的缓解策略。

Abstract: High-confidence errors in large language models are often treated as evidence of fragile internal inference. We study a different possibility: stable miscalibration, where a confident wrong answer remains locally stable under small perturbations. We combine two diagnostics: a label-aware output-level audit score that ranks domains by confidence variation and overconfident mistakes under a forced-answer baseline, and an internal sensitivity probe that measures hidden-state movement. On a multi-domain binary factual audit set, this audit score tracks where abstention-aware self-critique reduces decision loss, although direct labeled baselines rank the same gain more strongly. Internally, self-critical prompting consistently reduces hidden-state sensitivity across layers in three open-weight models. This supports prompt-induced local stabilization rather than a purely output-level abstention pattern, but it does not imply calibration: audit-defined overconfident errors are not clearly more locally sensitive than confidently correct answers, so some high-confidence errors may be stable and miscalibrated rather than simply fragile.

</details>


### [202] [Measuring Cross-Task Behavioral Consistency in Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13598)
*Amritesh Banerjee,Pranil Raichura*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出行为一致性度量（BCM），通过训练模型从智能体执行轨迹的行为特征预测任务成功，并计算轨迹特征归因向量的成对相似度，来量化智能体跨任务的行为一致性。在约 9000 条软件工程任务轨迹上发现，跨任务与任务内一致性是两个可分离的维度，且一致性与成功率不可互相化约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估几乎完全依赖成功率等结果指标，只能说明"是否成功"，无法反映智能体"行为是否一致"。作者认为跨任务的行为一致性是一个独立且可测量的属性，需要专门的方法来量化，以弥补结果指标的盲区。

Method: 提出行为一致性度量（BCM）：(1) 训练一个模型，以智能体执行轨迹中的行为特征为输入，预测任务是否成功；(2) 为每条轨迹推导特征归因向量（feature-attribution vector）；(3) 在同一个智能体系统内计算这些向量的平均成对相似度，作为一致性得分。实验覆盖六个语言模型智能体在软件工程任务上的约 9000 条轨迹，并区分跨任务一致性与任务内（同任务重复尝试）一致性，还引入任务难度恒定的任务内对照。

Result: 核心发现是跨任务一致性与任务内一致性是两个不同的轴且可能背离：部分系统在重复尝试同一任务时具备局部可复现性，但跨不同任务缺乏稳定策略（全局碎片化），而另一些系统在两种尺度上都一致；先前的同任务可复现性度量无法观察到此分离。一致性不可化约为成功率——成功率相近的系统一致性可能差异悬殊；在控制任务难度恒定的任务内对照下，前沿模型与开源模型之间的一致性差距依然存在。

Conclusion: BCM 可作为过程级可靠性信号，与结果指标互补。作者明确指出 BCM 有意义的前提条件，并主张在智能体评估中纳入行为一致性这一独立维度，以更全面地刻画智能体的可靠性。

Abstract: Agent evaluation relies almost entirely on outcome metrics such as success rate, which capture whether an agent succeeds but not how consistently it behaves. We argue that behavioral consistency across tasks is a distinct and measurable property, and we introduce the Behavioral Consistency Metric (BCM) to quantify it. BCM trains a model to predict task success from behavioral features of agent execution traces, derives a per-trajectory feature-attribution vector, and measures the mean pairwise similarity of these vectors within an agent system. Across roughly 9,000 trajectories from six language model agents on software engineering tasks, our central finding is that cross-task and within-task consistency are distinct axes that can diverge: some systems are locally reproducible, behaving similarly on repeated attempts at one task, yet globally fragmented, with no stable strategy across different tasks, while others are consistent at both scales. Prior work measures only same-task reproducibility and so cannot observe this separation. We further find that consistency is not reducible to success rate, since systems with comparable success can differ sharply in consistency, and that the frontier-versus-open-source consistency gap persists under a within-task control that holds task difficulty constant. We position BCM as a process-level reliability signal that complements outcome metrics, and we are explicit about the conditions under which it is meaningful.

</details>


### [203] [Cross-Disciplinary Taxonomy and Modeling of Misunderstanding Generation, Amplification, and Detection, from Pragmatics to AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13604)
*Babak Abbaschian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种分层化的误解分析框架：从九个领域的文献中归纳出11种精确的失败模式，定位到交际过程的8个分析层级，并区分其功能（生成分歧、放大分歧、决定是否检测修复），进而以形式化模型将信息与通信理论从信号传输扩展到意义重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沟通正从实时、面对面的互动转向AI中介的渠道，导致交际者更快地失去修复所需的资源，而新的误解检测手段未能同步建立，因此误解检测成为亟待解决的问题。

Method: 整合九个通常互不引用的研究领域的文献，归纳出11种精确失败模式；证明每种模式作用于交际过程的特定位置，从而得出8个源于文献的分析层级；其中8种机制主要生成分歧、2种主要放大既有分歧、1种决定分歧是否被检测与修复；进而对这8个层级进行形式化建模，将信息与通信理论从信号传输扩展到意义重建，并提供逐源的证据矩阵、编码手册及9个已分析的对话案例。

Result: 识别出11种精确失败模式，对应8个分析层级；8种机制生成分歧、2种放大分歧、1种治理检测与修复；形式化模型延伸了信息与通信理论，证据矩阵使每项评级可审计，并附编码手册和9个案例。

Conclusion: 现有的误解分类均未同时做到"将机制定位于过程的具体位置"与"按功能对机制进行分类"；本文的分层框架填补了这一空白，为在AI中介沟通中检测与修复误解提供了系统基础。

Abstract: Detection of misunderstanding is an urgent problem to solve because communication has moved away from real-time, in-person interaction and is increasingly handled by AI-mediated channels. This shift cuts communicators off from the resources repair depends on faster than new means of detection are being built. In this paper we analyse misunderstanding as a layered process in which a divergence is generated, may then be amplified, and is either detected and repaired or left to persist unnoticed. Consolidating accounts from nine fields of research that do not ordinarily cite one another, we identify eleven exact failure modes and show that each operates at a specific point in a communicative process rather than anywhere within it. Those points give eight analytical layers, derived from the literature rather than adopted from an existing model. Eight of the mechanisms primarily generate a divergence, two primarily amplify one already present, and one governs whether a divergence is detected and repaired. We model the eight layers formally, extending information and communication theory from the transmission of signals to the reconstruction of meaning, and we supply a source-by-source evidence matrix that makes every rating auditable, a coding manual, and nine analysed dialogue cases. No prior classification of misunderstanding both locates mechanisms at points in the process and types them by function.

</details>


### [204] [Active Perception for Embodied Disambiguation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13605)
*Yiwei Liu,Luwei Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种主动感知框架，通过机器人主动调整观察视角来获取缺失的物理证据，并结合大视觉-语言模型在持续观察、请求用户澄清与完成目标选择之间做决策，从而统一解决具身环境下的目标歧义问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然语言为机器人提供了灵活的交互接口，但具身环境中的目标歧义不仅源于用户意图不明确，还可能源于当前观测中缺少与任务相关的物理证据（如遮挡、视角受限、文字不可读、目标未被观测到）。现有交互式消歧方法主要依赖向用户提问获取信息，无法应对需要通过改变观测才能解决的歧义。

Method: 提出一种面向具身目标消歧的主动感知框架：以主动观测作为信息获取的主干，利用视觉-语言模型（VLM）根据累积的视觉证据和交互信息，决定下一步动作——继续观测、请求用户澄清或完成目标选择。主动观测既能直接恢复缺失的判别性证据，也能揭示物体名称、标签和语义属性，从而在仍需要时提升用户澄清的质量。

Result: 真实机器人实验表明，该框架将物理信息获取与用户意图澄清统一到一个具身消歧流程中，能够有效处理因遮挡、视角受限、文字不可读及目标未观测等导致的歧义。

Conclusion: 主动观测与用户澄清相结合，能够在统一的具身消歧框架内同时弥补物理证据缺失与用户意图模糊，实现更鲁棒的具身目标识别与选择。

Abstract: Natural language provides robots with a flexible task interface, but target ambiguity in embodied environments arises not only from user intent; it can also result from missing taskrelevant physical evidence in the current observation. Existing interactive disambiguation methods primarily obtain additional information by asking the user, whereas occlusion, restricted viewpoints, unreadable text, and unobserved targets require the robot to actively change its observation. We propose an active-perception framework for embodied target disambiguation that uses active observation as the backbone for information acquisition and uses a vision-language model to decide, on the basis of accumulated visual evidence and interaction information, whether to continue observing, request clarification, or complete target selection. Active observation can both directly recover missing discriminative evidence and reveal object names, labels, and semantic attributes, thereby improving user clarification when it remains necessary. Real-robot experiments show that the framework combines physical information acquisition and userintent clarification within a unified embodied disambiguation process.

</details>


### [205] [MobileMem: Learning from a Year of Mobile Experiences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13606)
*Xinle Deng,Yida Xue,Xiangyuan Ru,Haoming Xu,Shuofei Qiao,Mengru Wang,Yijun Chen,Buqiang Xu,Chen Jiang,Yuchen Eleanor Jiang,Lizhong Wang,Jianfeng Wang,Li Zeng,Haofen Wang,Guilin Qi,Huajun Chen,Ningyu Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 MobileMem，一个面向设备端长期记忆研究的基准与框架，基于一年规模的移动体验数据，通过知识引导的合成管线构造连贯、时间一致的长期轨迹，包含文本与多模态设置，覆盖多跳与时间推理、知识更新和隐式偏好推断，推动记忆从信息检索走向体验式智能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代 AI 智能体正从回答孤立问题转向能理解、记忆并持续学习用户经验的持久个人助理，这需要长期记忆来积累并利用用户特定的体验。然而现有基准不适用于现实移动场景，因为移动体验具有异构、多模态、动态演化且高度个人化的特点，缺乏能真实刻画长期记忆能力的评测。

Method: 提出 MobileMem 基准与框架，以一年规模的移动体验采集为基础，采用知识引导的合成管线（knowledge-grounded synthesis pipeline）从用户-App 会话中构建连贯且时间一致的长期轨迹；提供互补的文本与多模态设置，覆盖多跳推理、时间推理、知识更新与隐式偏好推断等能力，使智能体能够"记住过去、理解现在、适应未来"。

Result: MobileMem 通过对体验（而非孤立事实）进行建模，将长期记忆研究从单纯的信息检索推进到面向持续个人学习的体验式智能，并为评估设备端长期记忆能力提供了更贴合现实移动场景的基准与框架。

Conclusion: 现有长期记忆基准无法满足真实移动场景需求，MobileMem 通过建模时间一致、多模态、个人化的体验轨迹，填补了这一空白，推动长期记忆从信息检索向体验式智能演进，为持久个人助理的连续学习提供了评测基础。

Abstract: The next generation of AI agents is increasingly moving beyond systems that answer isolated questions toward persistent personal assistants that can understand, remember, and continuously learn from users' experiences. Such assistants require long-term memory to accumulate and leverage user-specific experiences over time, yet existing benchmarks remain inadequate for realistic mobile settings, where experiences are heterogeneous, multimodal, evolving, and deeply personal. We introduce MobileMem, a benchmark and framework for studying on-device long-term memory, grounded in a year-scale collection of mobile experiences. MobileMem employs a knowledge-grounded synthesis pipeline to construct coherent and temporally consistent long-horizon trajectories from user-app sessions. It provides complementary text and multimodal settings covering multi-hop and temporal reasoning, knowledge updating, and implicit preference inference. Specifically, MobileMem enables agents to remember the past, understand the present, and adapt to the future. By modeling experiences rather than isolated facts, MobileMem moves memory beyond information retrieval toward experiential intelligence for continuous personal learning.

</details>


### [206] [No Universal Signal Predicts Sample-Level LLM Regression under Version Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13607)
*Jia Sheng,Yiwei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究如何利用推理时可获取的信号来预测大语言模型版本更新导致的样本级性能回退（regression），并提出了一种统一增量价值测试来比较单模型信号与跨版本信号，为选择性回退策略提供依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿大模型频繁更新，聚合指标通常提升，但单个样本上仍可能出现"新模型答错、旧模型答对"的回退现象。聚合层面的提升掩盖了样本级回退风险，因此需要一种仅依赖推理时信号来预判哪些样本会发生回退的方法，以便在实际部署中规避风险。

Method: 作者在同一"增量价值测试"框架下比较两类推理时可获得的信号：单模型信号（置信度、logit 边际、注意力熵）与跨版本信号（输出 KL 散度、似然漂移、token 级 KL、表征漂移）。该测试以置信度作为基线，衡量每个信号相对基线的额外增益。实验覆盖三个任务族（多选题问答、数学推理、代码生成）的六个基准，以及六个模型更新对。

Result: 研究发现：(1) 信号有效性依赖任务类型——置信度在 MCQ 和较简单数学上最强，而似然/KL 信号在较难数学和代码任务上增益最频繁；(2) 在所有模型更新对上没有任何信号是普遍最优的；(3) 部分跨版本信号在置信度失效时仍具信息量，且无需标签，从而支持一种概念验证式的选择性回退策略，将高风险样本重新路由回旧模型。

Conclusion: 模型更新引发的样本级回退可以通过推理时信号进行预测，但最佳信号取决于具体任务与更新对。实践者可根据任务层面的规律，为特定更新选择合适的回退检测信号；跨版本信号在置信度失效时仍能提供信息，为无标签场景下的选择性回退提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Frontier LLMs are updated frequently and typically outperform their predecessors in aggregate. But aggregate gains say little about individual samples: an update can still cause sample-level regression, where a response correct under the old model becomes incorrect under the new one. This paper studies how to predict such regressions from signals available at inference time. We compare single-model signals (confidence, logit margin, attention entropy) against cross-version signals (output KL divergence, likelihood drift, token-level KL, representation drift) under a unified added-value test that isolates each signal's gain over a confidence baseline. Across six benchmarks in three task families (multiple-choice question answering, or MCQ; math reasoning; code generation) and six model update pairs, we find that (1) signal effectiveness is task-dependent: confidence is strongest on MCQ and simpler math, while likelihood/KL signals give the most frequent gains on harder math and code; (2) no signal is universally best across model updates either; and (3) some cross-version signals stay informative even when confidence fails, including without labels, which supports a proof-of-concept selective fallback that routes high-risk samples back to the old model. Practitioners can use these task-level patterns to choose which regression signal to trust for a given update. Code is available at https://github.com/jiashengsally/llm-regression-signals.

</details>


### [207] [Handover of In-Context Learning State Across Session Boundaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.14528)
*Masahiro Kato,Taka Kato*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文为基于大语言模型的应用建立会话交接（session handover）的理论与方法框架，将交接定义为任务相关的上下文内学习（ICL）状态的转移，研究在记忆约束下应保留何种信息、保留多少比特，并在不同回归模型下给出精确或渐近的记忆-误差界限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当上下文达到模型输入上限、应用重启或由另一智能体接续任务时，任务需要在新的会话中继续，应用必须决定从前一会话传递哪些信息。现有工作缺乏对“交接该保留什么、记忆需求如何随续接任务变化”的系统性理论与方法，因此本文旨在为交接决策提供形式化基础。

Method: 将交接形式化为任务相对上下文内学习（ICL）状态的转移，区分“精确恢复早期材料”与“保持目标分布”；在外生性（exogeneity）条件下，用可预测等价（predictive equivalence）刻画最粗的确定性充分交接，并给出固定长度比特需求；分离记忆约束、写入者与续接程序的影响，量化在未知下游查询之前提前写入的代价；提出三部分记录（精确存储决策与约束、用任务合理统计量概括重复证据、保留统计量无法覆盖的原始观测）；在Gauss线性回归下给出有限维精确交接与有限比特扰动界，在非参数回归下给出联系记忆与均方预测误差的上下界。

Result: 在外生性条件下，可预测等价刻画了最粗的确定性充分交接并给出固定长度比特需求；Gauss线性回归存在精确的有限维交接及有限比特扰动界；非参数回归的记忆与平方预测误差之间存在可量化的上下界；同时量化了在未知下游查询前预先写入的代价。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一套理论与实践方法，用于判定交接必须保留什么以及记忆需求如何取决于续接任务，为LLM应用中会话交接的信息选择与存储成本提供了可操作的依据。

Abstract: This study investigates the methodological and theoretical properties of session handover in applications that use large language models. A task may continue in a new session when the context reaches the model's input limit, when the application restarts, or when another agent is asked to finish the task. The application must then decide which information from the earlier session to pass on. We formulate handover as the transfer of a task-relative in-context learning (ICL) state and distinguish exact recovery of earlier material from preservation of the target distribution. Under an exogeneity condition, predictive equivalence characterizes the coarsest deterministic sufficient handover and gives a fixed-length bit requirement. The analysis isolates the effects of the memory constraint, the writer, and the continuation procedure, and quantifies the cost of writing before the realized downstream query is known. We propose a three-part record that stores decisions and constraints exactly, uses task-justified statistics for repeated evidence, and retains original observations whose effect is not preserved by those statistics. Gaussian linear regression gives an exact finite-dimensional handover and finite-bit perturbation bounds, while nonparametric regression gives upper and lower bounds that relate memory to squared prediction error. These results provide a theory and method for deciding what a handover must retain and how its memory requirement depends on the continuation task.

</details>


### [208] [Evaluating Agentic Learning Harness Capabilities Without Labels via the Scaling Hypothesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13608)
*Aryan Luthra,Kshitij Jain,Siddharth Arya,Bobby Filar,Anna Bertiger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需标注基准即可评估“LLM+检索/记忆”持续学习增强系统（harness）的框架：用更强教师模型提供稀疏修正，衡量学生模型向教师收敛的程度，并以“相对教师的提升（teacher-relative lift）”作为无标签场景下真实增强收益的代理指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络安全等运营场景中，有标签基准稀缺、过时且不具代表性，导致无法判断某个持续学习增强系统是否有效、或两个系统孰优孰劣。传统 LLM-as-a-judge 受限于其本身不优于被评估智能体，而蒸馏在稀疏、偶发且有偏的标签上不可靠。

Method: 基于缩放假设（scaling hypothesis）提出端到端评估框架：更强的教师模型向使用持续学习增强系统的较小学生模型提供稀疏采样修正；通过衡量学生模型随时间向教师模型收敛的程度来为增强系统打分。并在多类安全任务、多模型家族与多种增强设计上进行验证，同时对比 LLM-as-a-judge 的信号质量。

Result: 结果表明“相对教师的提升”与“相对留出黄金标准的提升”高度相关，证明 teacher-relative lift 可作为无标签情况下真实 uplift 的有效代理；而同等级别模型间的 LLM-as-a-judge 几乎不提供可用信号。由此推论，当人类提供同样稀疏、高精度的修正时，教师规模模型可经由同一增强系统获得改进。

Conclusion: 该框架为缺乏标签的真实运营环境提供了一种可用的增强系统评估方法，避免了依赖稀缺且有偏的基准标签；同时表明 LLM-as-a-judge 在评估同级别模型时失效，并暗示教师规模的模型加上人工稀疏高精度反馈同样可获提升。

Abstract: Agentic "Continual Learning Harnesses", systems that pair an LLM with retrieval or memory to improve from feedback without retraining, have shown growing value in cybersecurity. But their value is conventionally measured by gains against labeled benchmarks, an approach that often fails in operational security settings. Benchmark labels are scarce, stale, and unrepresentative, so a practitioner often cannot tell whether a given harness helps at all or which of two is better for their task. Traditional LLM-as-a-judge offers little signal because it is no stronger than the agent it evaluates, and distillation is unreliable on scarce, sporadic, and biased labels.
  We propose a framework for evaluating learning harnesses end-to-end without a labeled benchmark, grounded in the scaling hypothesis. A stronger teacher model provides sparsely sampled corrections to a smaller student with a continual learning harness. We score a harness by how much its student converges toward the teacher over time. Across security tasks, model families, and harness designs, we show that improvement relative to the teacher correlates with improvement relative to a held-out gold standard, validating teacher-relative lift as a proxy for true harness uplift when labels are absent. We further show that LLM-as-a-judge between similarly powered models yields no usable signal. These results suggest that a teacher-sized model can be improved through the same harness when humans provide the same kind of sparse, high-precision corrections.

</details>


### [209] [SemPlan: Benchmarking Structured Semantic Planning for LLM-Based Queries over Enterprise Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13612)
*Bruno Santos Teixeira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SemPlan Benchmark 通过一个含 1,800 个英葡双语案例的确定性合成基准，比较了四种企业数据自然语言接口架构（直接 SQL 生成 A1、工具代理 A2、语义请求+确定性规划 A3、澄清/有状态语义规划 A4），发现 A3 正确率最高（25.67%），但各架构在正确率、策略合规、成本与不确定性上呈权衡关系，不存在普适最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业数据的自然语言接口需将不明确的请求转化为受治理、可执行的行为，同时控制无效查询、策略失败、成本与不确定性；现有研究缺乏对这套架构设计空间的系统化、可复现评估。

Method: 构建确定性合成双语基准（英语与巴西葡萄牙语共 1,800 例，其中 1,200 例为冻结的科学评估子集）；在相同模型配置下对比四种架构：A1 直接 SQL 生成、A2 有界工具代理基线、A3 结构化语义请求后接确定性规划与执行、A4 澄清式/有状态语义规划变体；在 4,800 条主记录上进行配对正确率分析，并在预设 150 例稳定性子集上评估可重复性。

Result: 答案正确率整体偏低：A1 为 22.25%、A2 为 22.58%、A3 为 25.67%、A4 为 24.25%。A3 正确率最高，且在预设配对正确率分析中显著超过 A1、A2、A4；A1 策略正确率最高、不安全或无效率最低；A4 平均 API 成本最低、误拒率最低。稳定性子集上答案正确可重复率为 92.00%–98.67%。

Conclusion: 结果支持“权衡解释”而非普适排序：增加结构约束改变了失效模式与效率，但并未单调提升正确率，也未能解决歧义和多轮状态一致性问题。

Abstract: Natural-language interfaces to enterprise data must translate underspecified requests into governed, executable behavior while controlling invalid queries, policy failures, cost, and nondeterminism. SemPlan Benchmark evaluates this architectural design space with a deterministic synthetic bilingual benchmark containing 1,800 cases in English and Brazilian Portuguese; 1,200 cases form the frozen scientific evaluation subset. Four architectures are compared under the same model configuration: direct SQL generation (A1), a bounded tool-agent baseline (A2), structured semantic-request generation followed by deterministic planning and execution (A3), and a clarification/stateful semantic-plan variant (A4). Across 4,800 primary records, answer correctness was low in absolute terms: 22.25% for A1, 22.58% for A2, 25.67% for A3, and 24.25% for A4. A3 had the highest observed correctness and significantly exceeded A1, A2, and A4 in the pre-specified paired correctness analysis, while A1 retained the highest policy-correct rate and the lowest unsafe-or-invalid rate. A4 had the lowest mean API cost and lowest false-refusal rate. On a preselected 150-case stability subset, answer-correct repeatability ranged from 92.00% to 98.67%. The results support a trade-off interpretation rather than a universal ranking: additional structural constraints changed failure modes and efficiency, but did not monotonically improve correctness or solve ambiguity and multi-turn state consistency.

</details>


### [210] [How Compliant is Sepsis Treatment? An Expert-Guided Neuro-symbolic Pipeline for Generating Clinical Compliance Insights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13617)
*Himanshu Tripathi,Kaushik Roy,Subash Neupane,Shahram Rahimi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种专家引导的神经-符号混合流程，用于核查临床护理是否符合循证方案：大语言模型仅负责将杂乱的药物与微生物学字符串语义归一化到固定临床词表，而由Sugeno模糊推理系统对归一化事件进行分级评分推理；在2,438例MIMIC-IV脓毒症病例上，识别出抗生素时机是最关键失效点、第一小时依从性不佳及乳酸复查流失率等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证临床护理是否遵循循证方案本质上是一个神经-符号问题，但安全关键场景下任何单一范式（纯神经或纯符号）都不足：纯神经网络缺乏可解释、可验证的推理，纯符号系统难以处理真实电子病历中杂乱的非结构化字符串。因此需要一种既能可靠解析文本、又能进行可审计规则推理的混合方案。

Method: 提出专家引导流水线：(1) 将大语言模型严格约束在语义归一化任务上，把杂乱的药物和微生物学字符串映射到固定临床词表；(2) 用Sugeno模糊推理系统对归一化后的临床事件进行推理，编码八条Surviving Sepsis Campaign集束化规则，并将二值判定替换为[0,1]区间内的分级得分；(3) 在2,438例MIMIC-IV v3.1脓毒症发作病例上应用评估。

Result: 抗生素给药时机是最关键的失效环节（平均得分0.24，仅13%在一小时内完成）；Hour-1集束化表现欠佳（平均依从率36.7%）；51%的乳酸升高病例存在复查流失；不同依从性分组的ICU住院时长存在描述性差异（3.8天 vs 5.1天）。

Conclusion: 通过将大语言模型约束在语义归一化、由模糊逻辑负责推理的神经-符号分工，可以在安全关键的临床依从性核查中兼顾文本解析鲁棒性与推理可解释性，并能有效定位脓毒症集束化护理中的具体失效环节，为质量改进提供依据。

Abstract: Verifying whether clinical care follows evidence-based protocols is a natural neuro-symbolic problem, yet the safety-critical setting defeats either paradigm alone. We present an expert-guided pipeline that constrains a large language model strictly to semantic normalization, mapping messy drug and microbiology strings onto a fixed clinical vocabulary, while a Sugeno fuzzy inference system reasons over the normalized events. The fuzzy layer encodes eight Surviving Sepsis Campaign bundle rules and replaces binary judgments with graded scores in [0,1]. Applied to 2,438 MIMIC-IV v3.1 sepsis episodes, it surfaces antibiotic timing as the most critical breakdown (mean 0.24, 13% within one hour), Hour-1 underperformance (mean 36.7%), a 51% elevated-lactate drop-off, and descriptive differences in ICU stay across compliance groups (3.8 versus 5.1 days).

</details>


### [211] [Algorithm Design and Physician Liability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13618)
*Shujie Luan,Shubhranshu Singh,Tinglong Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究美国一项规定医疗提供者因依赖存在群体差异的算法而做出错误临床决策时须承担责任的规则，如何影响AI公司的算法设计（群体间准确率）与医生的AI使用决策。核心发现：责任规则会导致医生对弱势群体更少使用AI（非单调），而强制要求群体间准确率相等反而可能同时伤害两个群体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一临床算法在不同患者群体间准确率不均，随着AI在临床决策中普及，这种差异引发的公平性担忧日益加剧。美国引入的责任规则使提供者需对其依赖差异算法导致的错误决策负责，但该规则对算法设计与医生使用行为的实际影响尚不明确。

Method: 构建博弈论模型：AI公司针对两个患者群体设计算法，提高弱势群体准确率的成本更高；医生（作为最终责任承担者）在权衡临床不确定性降低与预期责任风险后，决定是否使用AI。分析责任强度对均衡算法设计与AI使用决策的影响。

Result: 责任规则可诱发差异性AI使用：医生可能整体减少AI使用，且在中间责任水平区间对弱势患者使用AI更少。该效应非单调——随着责任增大，医生对弱势患者的AI使用先下降，后因公司重新分配投资以缩小差异或转向等准确率设计而回升。强制要求群体间算法准确率相等反而会因扭曲公司投资激励与医生均衡使用决策，同时伤害两个群体。

Conclusion: 旨在消除算法差异的责任规则会引发复杂的非单调行为，可能导致对弱势群体的AI使用反而下降；而简单的“等准确率”强制政策可能适得其反，同时损害所有群体。政策制定需考虑算法设计投资与医生使用决策的联动机制。

Abstract: A single clinical algorithm can deliver unequal accuracy across patient groups, and concern about such disparity has grown as artificial intelligence (AI) spreads through clinical decision-making. In response, a liability rule introduced in the United States holds healthcare providers responsible when their reliance on disparate algorithms contributes to erroneous clinical decisions. We examine how such liability considerations reshape (i) an AI firm's algorithm design decisions that drive group-specific accuracy and (ii) a physician's decisions to use AI in healthcare delivery. The AI firm designs an algorithm for two patient groups, and improving accuracy for the disadvantaged group is more costly. The physician (who remains the accountable decision-maker) then decides whether to consult AI, weighing the reduction in clinical uncertainty against expected liability exposure when AI errors disproportionately affect the disadvantaged group. We find the liability rule can induce disparate use of AI: the physician may reduce AI use overall and, over an intermediate range of liability, rely on AI less for disadvantaged patients. The effect is non-monotone. As liability increases, the physician's use of AI for disadvantaged patients first declines, then rises as the firm reallocates investment toward reducing disparity or switches to an equal-accuracy design. Mandating equal algorithmic accuracy across patient groups can then inadvertently harm both groups, because a uniform accuracy requirement distorts the firm's investment incentives and the physician's equilibrium AI-use decisions.

</details>


### [212] [Your Probabilistic JEPA Is Secretly a Hidden Markov Model: A State-Space Interpretation of Joint-Embedding Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13621)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文证明全时序的预测信息瓶颈 VJEPA（PIB-VJEPA）在计算结构上与隐马尔可夫模型（HMM）等价，并提出 Markov-Chain JEPA（MCJEPA）作为具体实现，通过可学习的转移矩阵保证多时域 Chapman–Kolmogorov 一致性，从而赋予时序 JEPA 一个有原则的状态空间解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时序 JEPA（联合嵌入预测架构）与隐马尔可夫模型在结构上的联系尚未被阐明；作者希望通过建立二者之间明确的形式化对应关系，为 JEPA 提供坚实的概率与状态空间基础，并据此改进其多步预测的一致性与可解释性。

Method: 作者揭示 HMM 的三个角色（从观测推断隐状态信念、马尔可夫转移、发射回观测空间）与 PIB-VJEPA 的组件（随机上下文编码器、概率预测器、解码器/逆目标编码器/隐式条件）之间的对应，划分出 4 个逐级增强的对应层次，并给出序列级 HMM 精确等价的充分条件。随后引入 MCJEPA，用学习的转移矩阵替代隐式预测器；在有限时间齐次情形下，矩阵幂保证精确的多时域 Chapman–Kolmogorov 一致性。作者进一步扩展了条件离散转移、连续状态马尔可夫核、连续时间动力学，并将确定性时序 JEPA 视为退化的 Dirac 核特例。最后通过受控实验验证转移复合、滤波解释、合成过程上的预测马尔可夫化，以及 JEPA 隐式预测与 HMM 式序列学习的区别。

Result: 实验支持了转移复合、滤波解释，在已知合成过程中实现了预测马尔可夫化，并成功区分了 JEPA 隐式预测与 HMM 式序列学习；预测信息瓶颈学习可被解释为寻求紧致的预测状态，其中压缩促进最小性，而残差可预测性检验充分性。

Conclusion: 全时序 PIB-VJEPA 与 HMM 之间存在多层次的精确对应关系，MCJEPA 通过学习转移矩阵实现了等价且多时域一致的 HMM 式结构，从而为时序 JEPA 提供了有原则的状态空间解释。

Abstract: A hidden Markov model (HMM) combines three roles: inference of a hidden-state belief from observations, propagation through a Markov transition, and emission back to observation space. We show that full, time-indexed Predictive Information Bottleneck VJEPA (PIB-VJEPA) exposes the same computational structure: a stochastic context encoder plays the role of an amortized filtering distribution, a probabilistic predictor defines latent-state dynamics, and a decoder, inverse target encoder, or induced implicit conditional supplies the emission direction. We distinguish 4 progressively stronger levels of correspondence and give sufficient conditions for exact sequence-level HMM equivalence. To make the connection concrete, we introduce Markov-Chain JEPA (MCJEPA), which replaces the latent predictor by a learned transition matrix; in the finite time-homogeneous case, matrix powers guarantee exact multi-horizon Chapman--Kolmogorov consistency. Conditioned discrete-state transitions, continuous-state Markov kernels, and continuous-time dynamics extend this construction, while deterministic temporal JEPA appears as a degenerate Dirac-kernel special case. We further interpret predictive information-bottleneck learning as seeking a compact predictive state: compression promotes minimality, while residual predictability tests sufficiency. Controlled experiments support transition composition, the filtering interpretation, predictive Markovization in a known synthetic process, and the distinction between JEPA latent prediction and HMM-style sequence learning. Together, these results give temporal JEPA a principled state-space interpretation.

</details>


### [213] [ARC: Fair Relative Advantage Comparison in Open-Ended Real-World Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13622)
*Yongqi Tong,Tan Li Hui Faith,Choy Zhen Wen Marcus,Zhou Jin,Kewei Fu,Jiang-Ming Yang,Jianshe Li,Xin Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对开放式真实交互中多种行为均有效的场景，指出现有基于分组的强化学习因组内样本行为不可比而导致奖励偏好扭曲优化方向，提出“奖励公平问题”并给出 ARC（基于条件化的优势正则化）训练方案，配合新的 inter 交互范式和 inter-86K 数据集，显著提升工具调用基准并大幅降低首token延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放式真实世界交互允许智能体采取多种不同但都有效的行为（直接回答、请求澄清、报告进度、行动前确认等），这打破了分组强化学习“同一组内 rollout 行为可比”的核心假设。若奖励模型对交互风格存在偏好，会导致相对优势计算失真，使优化偏向奖励偏好的行为而非当前情境下最合适的行为。作者将其形式化为“奖励公平问题”，指出开放式交互学习的核心瓶颈不仅是奖励方式，更在于行为是否被公平比较。

Method: 提出 ARC（Advantage Regularization via Conditioning），通过策略条件化的 rollout 分组恢复更公平的相对比较，并结合混合奖励与熵正则化。为研究该方法，设计了 inter 交互范式，将用户可见的沟通与潜在推理、工具使用解耦，实现响应式、可操控且执行感知的交互；并提供标注与蒸馏流水线，构建策略标注的 inter-86K 训练语料，用于监督学习和强化学习训练。

Result: ARC 显著提升了核心 τ/τ² 工具调用基准的性能；inter 范式相对 think 风格基线将首 token 延迟从 4.91 秒降至 1.27 秒。

Conclusion: 结果表明，开放式交互学习的核心瓶颈不仅在于如何奖励智能体，更在于其行为在比较时是否公平。ARC 实现与 inter-86K 训练数据将开源发布。

Abstract: Open-ended real-world interaction admits multiple valid behaviors: an agent may answer directly, ask for clarification, provide progress updates, or confirm before acting. This flexibility breaks a core assumption behind group-based RL: rollouts compared within a group are no longer guaranteed to be behaviorally comparable. As a result, reward-model preferences over interaction style can distort relative advantages and steer optimization toward reward-preferred behaviors rather than context-appropriate ones. We formalize this as a \textit{reward fairness problem} and propose \textbf{ARC} (Advantage Regularization via Conditioning), a training recipe that restores fairer relative comparison through strategy-conditioned rollout grouping, together with hybrid rewards and entropy regularization. We study ARC in our proposed \inter, a novel paradigm for responsive, steerable, and execution-aware user-agent interaction that decouples user-visible communication from latent reasoning and tool use. \inter\ also provides the annotation and distillation pipeline for constructing \inter-86K, our strategy-annotated training corpus for supervised and RL training. Empirically, ARC substantially strengthens the core $τ/τ^2$ tool-use benchmarks, while \inter\ reduces time-to-first-token from 4.91s to 1.27s relative to a think-style baseline. Together, these results suggest that a central bottleneck in open-ended interactive learning is not only how agents are rewarded, but whether their behaviors are compared fairly in the first place. The ARC implementation and \inter-86K training data will be released.

</details>


### [214] [Reward Machines for Signal Temporal Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13625)
*Alper Kamil Bozkurt,Shangtong Zhang,Yuichi Motai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于自动机的 STL 控制合成方法，通过构造定时交替自动机并为强化学习提供马尔可夫奖励，解决了长时序、嵌套时序算子规格下基于鲁棒性奖励导致的状态空间扩张问题，实验表明其鲁棒性与满足率优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代自主与 AI 系统日益复杂，缺乏精确完整的模型，使基于优化的控制合成不可行，从而催生基于学习（强化学习）的方法。现有工作将 STL 鲁棒性分数作为强化学习奖励，但鲁棒性依赖完整执行历史，对于具有任意嵌套时序算子的长时序规格会导致状态空间不可控地扩张，难以学习。

Method: 提出一种新颖的基于自动机的方法：从给定 STL 规格构造定时交替自动机（timed alternating automaton），将自动机位置和时钟赋值扩充进状态空间，并从自动机的接受条件中推导奖励，从而得到适合强化学习框架的高效记忆机制与马尔可夫奖励。

Result: 经验实验表明，相比使用基于鲁棒性奖励的现有方法，本文方法学习到的策略能获得更高的鲁棒性分数和更高的规格满足率。

Conclusion: 基于定时交替自动机的方法能够为 STL 规格下的强化学习控制合成提供高效记忆与马尔可夫奖励，有效应对长时序复杂规格，并在鲁棒性与满足率上优于基于鲁棒性奖励的现有方法。

Abstract: Signal temporal logic (STL) provides a formal language for specifying real-time properties of real-valued observations, along with a quantitative robustness score for monitoring satisfaction. Control synthesis from STL specifications is of interest since manual controller design becomes infeasible as real-world systems grow in complexity. Moreover, many modern autonomous and AI-enabled systems lack accurate and complete system models, which makes optimization-based synthesis approaches unsuitable and motivates learning-based control. Prior work uses STL robustness scores as rewards in reinforcement learning (RL) to obtain control policies satisfying given specifications; however, robustness depends on execution history, leading to intractable state space expansion for general long-horizon specifications with arbitrarily nested temporal operators. This work introduces a novel automata-based approach that provides an efficient memory mechanism and associated Markovian rewards suitable for RL frameworks. Our approach constructs a timed alternating automaton from the given STL specifications, augments the state space with automaton locations and clock valuations, and derives rewards from the automaton acceptance condition. We empirically demonstrate that our approach learns policies that achieve higher robustness scores and satisfaction rates than those learned by existing approaches using robustness-based rewards.

</details>


### [215] [A Calibrated Test of Internal Action Maps: State Signals Without Global Affine Closure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13626)
*Dekun Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统检验了隐藏状态信号在“可解码性/因果可用性”与“可复用的动作映射（闭包）”之间是否可分离，并在 Qwen3-4B 与有限世界载体上发现：状态可用性、因果使用、局部几何与可复用闭包四者相互独立，几何分支的证据虽成立但具有边界性而非普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期以来关于“隐藏状态是否编码了世界状态/能否被线性探针解码”的争论中，一个信号可以被解码或因果使用，却不一定支持一个可复用的动作映射。作者希望厘清“状态可用（availability）、因果可用（causal use）、局部几何结构与可复用动作映射（reusable closure）”这些性质是绑定的还是可以分离的，从而揭示线性表征假设的适用边界。

Method: 作者将各项检验组织为“证据格”（evidence lattice），并设置多层关卡：一步（one-step）、组合（composition）、逆（inverse）、解码（decoding）、交换律（commutativity）。几何分支在一个已知仿射 S_5 载体上验证；在训练后的 Qwen/Qwen3-4B 上，用冻结的末 token h28 仿射映射做留出实体（held-entity）误差与测试域内交叉拟合（cross-fit）的对比，进行七次随机化实体划分与映射几何分析，考察更早的 h4/h16 层，并用同一冻结检查点再生三个匹配的干预数据集观察 h28/h36 的因果效应；还尝试结果感知（outcome-aware）重拟合和加权，并检验学到的有限世界是否保留相对代数信号或共享图表。

Result: 在仿射 S_5 载体上，所有留出源折叠（held-source folds）均通过一步、组合、逆、解码和交换律关卡；结构曲率与留出域共轭使误差单调上升，但在 30 个最强单元中仅 23 个翻转闭包关卡，说明校准是边界性的而非普适的。在 Qwen3-4B 上，冻结的末 token h28 仿射映射留出实体平均误差为 .519，而测试域内交叉拟合为 .398；七次随机实体划分与映射几何不支持“纯实体特异”解释。h4/h16 层拟合一步迁移更好，但 h4 冲突状态解码较弱，词汇控制项仍未解决。三个匹配干预数据集仅显示 h28/h36 的因果效应。结果感知重拟合将 h28 一步误差改善至 .474（加权为 .469），但没有任何重拟合通过组合关卡。学到的有限世界保留了相对代数信号或共享图表，却缺乏留出源的仿射闭包。

Conclusion: 在测试的载体范围内，状态可用性、因果使用、局部几何与可复用闭包是可以分离的。该结论受限于单一预训练模型、采样的末 token 层、两个有限世界以及所测试的仿射或诊断函数类，因此是边界性而非普适性结论。

Abstract: A hidden state signal can be decodable or causally usable without supporting a reusable action map. We test whether action maps fitted without a source reach its natural post-action activation and compose. We organize the tests as an evidence lattice and validate the geometric branch on a known affine S_5 carrier: all held-source folds pass one-step, composition, inverse, decoding, and commutativity gates. Structured curvature and held-domain conjugacy raise error monotonically, but only 23/30 strongest cells flip a closure gate, bounding rather than universalizing calibration. In post-trained Qwen/Qwen3-4B, frozen final-token h28 affine maps have mean held-entity error .519, versus .398 for within-test-domain cross-fit. Seven randomized entity splits and map geometry do not support a purely entity-specific account. Earlier h4/h16 layers fit one-step transitions better, but h4 conflict-state decoding is weak and lexical controls remain unresolved. Three matched intervention datasets regenerated from one frozen checkpoint show causal effects only at h28/h36. Outcome-aware refitting improves h28 one-step error to .474 (.469 with weighting), yet no refit passes composition. Learned finite worlds likewise preserve relative algebraic signals or shared charts without held-source affine closure. Within the tested carriers, state availability, causal use, local geometry, and reusable closure are separable. The result is limited to one pretrained model, sampled final-token layers, two finite worlds, and the tested affine or diagnostic function classes.

</details>


### [216] [Exploring ESC Winners with Nested Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13630)
*Anurag Sharma,Marcel Nöhre,Gerd Stumme*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ConceptFlow 是一个兼容 scikit-learn 的 Python 库，用于形式概念分析（FCA），可从多值形式背景构建并渲染嵌套线图；作者将其应用于 1975–2025 年欧洲歌唱大赛（Eurovision）冠军，揭示投票模式与音乐特征之间的蕴含关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有形式概念分析工具在处理多值形式背景时，概念标度化、因子格构造以及子直积的交互式可视化往往需要多步手工操作，缺乏一个集成化、与 scikit-learn 兼容的自动化流程。作者希望提供统一的工具，将多值背景的标度化、格计算与嵌套线图渲染整合在一起，从而更便捷地探索跨多个概念标度的属性蕴含关系。

Method: ConceptFlow 接收一个多值形式背景及其属性到概念标度的划分，依次执行：概念标度化（conceptual scaling）、计算各因子格（factor lattices）、识别对应子直积（subdirect product）中的填充节点（filled nodes），并生成交互式可视化。该库遵循 scikit-learn 接口约定（fit/transform 风格），便于与现有机器学习工作流集成。在案例中，外标度从地域、文化、历史、政治维度刻画投票支持，内标度以速度（tempo）和调性（key）刻画音乐特征。

Result: 将 ConceptFlow 应用于 1975–2025 年 Eurovision 冠军数据后，生成的嵌套线图揭示了跨两个标度的蕴涵关系，表明获胜歌曲被投票支持的方式与其共享的音乐属性（速度和调性）之间存在依赖关系，即投票模式蕴含（或受约束于）特定的音乐特征。

Conclusion: ConceptFlow 为多值形式背景的 FCA 分析提供了易用、自动化的嵌套线图构造与可视化能力，并通过 Eurovision 案例证明了其在发现跨标度属性蕴涵方面的实用性，有助于从复杂多维度数据中揭示隐藏的结构性依赖。

Abstract: We present ConceptFlow, a scikit-learn-compatible Python library for Formal Concept Analysis that constructs and renders nested line diagrams from many-valued formal contexts. Given a many-valued context and a partition of its attributes into conceptual scales, ConceptFlow performs conceptual scaling, computes the factor lattices, identifies filled nodes of the corresponding subdirect product, and produces an interactive visualization.
  We apply ConceptFlow to the winners of the Eurovision Song Contest from 1975 to 2025, exploring relationships between voting patterns and musical characteristics. Voting support is captured by an outer scale spanning regional, cultural, historical, and political dimensions, while an inner scale captures musical characteristics via tempo and key. The resulting nested line diagram reveals implications across both scales, exposing dependencies between how winning entries were voted for and the musical properties they share.

</details>


### [217] [Ontology-Grounded Project Memory for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13662)
*James Adam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍 MOOSEDev，一个为编码代理提供结构化、本体论支撑项目记忆的系统，通过知识图谱与 MCP 接口记录架构决策、经验教训、约束与理由，并用神经符号引擎 MOOSE 进行查询，在超集、集合完备性与否定类问题上大幅优于向量记忆基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码代理已成为许多软件项目生成新代码的主要手段，代码变更速度极快，导致难以追踪这些变更背后的原因（架构决策、约束、理由等）。现有向量记忆工具擅长相关性检索，但在需要精确推理的问题（如记录是否已被取代、集合是否完备、否定查询）上表现不佳。

Method: 构建 MOOSEDev 系统：用知识图谱承载结构化项目记忆，记录含生命周期状态、出处（provenance）与取代（supersession）链接；通过 Model Context Protocol (MCP) 接口向代理暴露；查询经由 MOOSE——一个将符号层作为主要推理基底的专有神经符号引擎处理。评估上，在中性公开语料库 835 条带类型记录上与生产级向量记忆工具对比，并开展自身代码库的时间线提交历史引导及预注册的实地试验。

Result: 在 supersession、集合完备性与否定问题上，MOOSEDev 几乎完整返回期望答案集（0.98–1.00），而基线的 top-k 检索仅返回 6%–27%；在相关性召回与 token 成本上两者基本相当。

Conclusion: 将结构化、本体论支撑的符号记忆作为编码代理的推理基底，能在需要精确集合语义与逻辑推理的任务上显著优于纯向量记忆方法，同时保持相似的相关性召回与成本，证明了该架构的实用价值。

Abstract: Coding agents have become the primary means of generating new code in many software projects, and the resulting velocity of changes makes keeping track of the reasons behind those changes challenging. This paper introduces MOOSEDev, a system designed to give coding agents structured, ontology-grounded project memory. The system captures architectural decisions, lessons, constraints, and rationales in a knowledge graph exposed to agents via a Model Context Protocol (MCP) interface. Records carry lifecycle status, provenance, and supersession links, queryable via MOOSE, a proprietary neurosymbolic engine that treats the symbolic layer as the primary reasoning substrate. We compared MOOSEDev against a production vector-memory tool on a neutral public corpus of 835 typed records. MOOSEDev returned the expected answer set essentially in full (0.98-1.00) on supersession, set-completeness, and negation questions, whereas the baseline's top-k retrieval surfaced between 6% and 27%. Conversely, relevance recall and token cost were largely equivalent between the two systems. We also describe a temporal commit-history bootstrap of our own codebase, a pre-registered live trial, and lessons learned.

</details>


### [218] [Second Thought: Reasoning in Parallel as LLM Agents Act and Observe](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13667)
*Zhensu Sun,Chengran Yang,Yunbo Lyu,Jieke Shi,David Lo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Second Thought，一种免训练的推理加速框架。它利用 ReAct 智能体中 Action/Observation 阶段的“推理空闲窗口”，在每次 Thought 阶段结束的瞬间分出四个辅助分支并行解码推理，待环境观测返回时再合并这些“第二思考”，从而在不占用主线程串行解码路径的前提下增加思考量。在三个智能体基准、三种推理 LLM 上，该方法降低了所有九个（模型,基准）组合的平均轮数，并在其中六个上最高减少 43% 的主线程解码（平均约 20%），且 Pass@1 大多无显著变化或显著提升；相比将等量预算强制加回主线程的对照，它在四个适用设置下 Pass@1 更高、串行解码更少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 ReAct 范式智能体仅在 Thought 阶段进行思考，而在把动作序列化并等待环境反馈（Action 与 Observation）期间，模型的推理能力处于“冻结”状态。作者将这段反复出现的间隔定义为“推理空闲窗口”，并探究能否利用这一并行窗口承载服务于未来轮次的额外推理，以在不拖慢主线程顺序生成的代价下增强智能体推理能力。

Method: 提出 Second Thought，一个免训练的推理框架：每当主循环 Thought 阶段结束的瞬间，分出四个辅助分支；这些分支与主循环（Action→环境→Observation）并发解码，生成额外的思考；当环境观测返回时，将这些并行生成的思考合并回主线程，供后续轮次使用。通过把新增推理“搬离”主线程的串行解码路径，实现推理与等待的重叠，并以汇聚机制复用辅助思考。为验证并行推理的真实收益，还设计了将等量计算预算强制压回主线程自身推理的计算量匹配对照。

Result: 在三个智能体基准和三种推理 LLM 上，Second Thought 在所有九个（模型,基准）组合中都降低了平均轮数；在九组中的六组上减少了主线程解码，最高达 43%（这些情况下平均约 20%），另有一组基本不变；Pass@1 在九组中的七组无显著变化，另两组的显著差异分别为 +12.4 和 +10.2 点。相较计算量匹配的对照（把等效预算强加给主线程自身推理），在对照适用的全部四个设置下，Second Thought 以少 1.3 到 3.2 的串行解码获得了严格更高的 Pass@1。

Conclusion: Second Thought 证明，ReAct 智能体中 Action/Observation 阶段的推理空闲窗口可以被并行利用来承载额外推理，且这种做法优于把同等预算压回主线程顺序推理。该方法作为免训练框架，能在不牺牲准确率（甚至提升）的前提下，显著降低轮数和主线程顺序解码量，为智能体的高效推理调度提供了新思路。

Abstract: LLM agents in the ReAct paradigm alternate between reasoning, acting, and observing, but deliberate reasoning is confined to the Thought phase: while the agent serializes an action and waits for the environment, its reasoning is frozen. We identify this recurring interval for Action and Observation as a reasoning idle window and ask whether it can host additional reasoning in parallel that serves future turns. Therefore, we propose Second Thought, a training-free inference framework that forks four auxiliary branches the instant each Thought phase concludes, decodes them concurrently with the main loop, and merges the generated thoughts back when the environment observation arrives. In this way, Second Thought relocates the added reasoning off the main thread's sequential decoding path. Across three agentic benchmarks and three reasoning LLMs, Second Thought lowers the average turn count in all nine (model,benchmark) pairs and reduces main thread decoding in six of them by up to 43% (roughly 20% on average among those settings), while leaving it essentially unchanged in a seventh; Pass@1 shows no significant change in seven of nine pairs and the two significant differences are +12.4 and +10.2 points. Against a compute-matched control that forces an equivalent budget onto the main thread's own reasoning, it attains strictly higher Pass@1 with 1.3 to 3.2 less sequential decoding in all four settings where the control applies.

</details>


### [219] [Learning to Assemble Novel Structures with Unfamiliar Parts under Semantic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13684)
*Jonghyuk Park,Alex Lascarides,Subramanian Ramamoorthy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号架构，使智能体能够在部署后通过与用户的具身对话和任务演示，学习组装包含新语义约束的新结构，并在模拟玩具卡车组装领域验证了自然语言传达语义约束比仅靠演示或仅命名零件更高效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在部署后可能遭遇训练时未见过的结构语义约束（即哪些零件类型和特征能组成有效结构），且最初并不了解相关结构和零件概念，因此需要能在任务执行过程中通过与用户互动主动获取并利用这些知识。

Method: 设计一种神经符号架构，以具身对话中编码的自然语言符号证据和密集视觉观察为输入，在模拟玩具卡车组装领域中进行学习；对比不同知识来源（自然语言语义约束、任务演示、仅零件命名）对在线适应效率的影响。

Result: 实验表明，通过自然语言传达语义约束（如“自卸卡车有翻斗”）比仅依赖任务演示和/或仅通过自然语言命名零件，能实现更高的数据效率在线适应。

Conclusion: 在部署后遭遇未知语义约束的场景下，利用自然语言显式传递结构化语义知识能显著提升智能体组装学习的数据效率，凸显了对话中语义约束沟通对神经符号系统在线适应的重要价值。

Abstract: This paper describes a neurosymbolic architecture for learning to assemble novel structures using evidence from embodied conversations and task demonstrations. We focus on scenarios where an agent encounters, after deployment, semantic constraints on structures--in other words, constraints as to which part types and features make valid structures--that were not available during training, and where it is initially unaware of the relevant structure and component part concepts. The agent must acquire and exploit such knowledge through user interactions while attempting assembly. We study this setting in a simulated toy truck assembly domain, learning from symbolic evidence encoded in natural language and from dense visual observations. Our experiments show that communicating semantic constraints through natural language (e.g., "dump trucks have a dumper") yields more data-efficient online adaptation than relying only on task demonstrations and/or only naming the parts through natural language.

</details>


### [220] [Coverage Aware Active Evaluation for Failure Discovery with Paired Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13719)
*Anjali Parashar,Rachel Luo,Apoorva Sharma,Sushant Veer,Edward Schmerling,Carson Sobolewski,Mingxin Yu,Chuchu Fan,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自适应失败发现方法，通过结合代理系统（模拟器/低保真系统）评估与有限目标系统测试结果，用控制变量风格的残差建模修正代理失败信号，并以支持感知的互信息目标引导场景选择，从而在真实目标系统中高效发现多样且严重的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主系统会以罕见且异质的方式失败，在有限测试预算下难以在真实世界发现失败。虽然模拟器等廉价代理可被大量采样，但代理失败常因 sim-to-real 与 system-to-system 差距而无法迁移到真实世界，因此核心挑战是如何有效利用代理系统信息来准确预测目标系统的严重失败。

Method: 提出自适应失败发现方法：1）通过控制变量（control-variate）启发的残差建模，学习目标风险局部预测器，以修正代理失败信号；2）将该预测器与支持感知（support-aware）的互信息目标结合，在偏好真实、有充分支持区域的同时，扩大对不同失败模式的覆盖，从而指导目标系统测试的场景选择。

Result: 在自动驾驶、机械臂操作和四足机器人速度跟踪任务中，该方法发现的失败数量达到随机采样和主动学习基线的 2 倍，并包括竞争方法遗漏的严重且多样的失败。

Conclusion: 所提方法能有效利用代理系统信息，在有限目标系统测试预算下显著提升失败发现的效率与多样性，优于随机采样和主动学习基线。

Abstract: Autonomous systems can fail in rare and heterogeneous ways, making real-world failure discovery difficult under limited testing budgets. Although cheaper proxies such as simulators, lower-fidelity systems, or related policies can be sampled extensively to find failures, proxy failures often do not transfer to the real world due to sim-to-real and system-to-system gaps. The key challenge is therefore to effectively leverage proxy system information for accurate prediction of severe target system failures. We propose an adaptive failure discovery method that combines proxy evaluations with limited target system results to guide scenario selection for target system testing. Our method learns a local predictor of target risk by correcting proxy failure signals using control-variate-inspired residual modeling. To find failures that are both likely and diverse, we combine this predictor with a support-aware mutual-information objective that favors realistic, well-supported regions while expanding coverage across failure modes. Across autonomous driving, manipulation, and quadruped velocity-tracking tasks, our method discovers up to 2$\times$ as many failures as random sampling and active-learning baselines, including severe and diverse failures missed by competing methods.

</details>


### [221] [Explanation Multiplicity: Circuit-Level Interpretability Evidence Does Not Survive Defensible Analytic Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13754)
*Ajay Pravin Mahale*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究检验电路发现方法能否作为欧盟AI法案所要求的高风险系统技术文档证据。通过大规模预注册实验（GPT-2 small、15,840项规格、7,561项产生主张），发现同一系统在相同工具、不同合理设置下，导出的合规陈述在73.2%的规格对之间翻转，且底层电路结构近乎不相交、功能上无相关，表明电路发现目前尚不足以作为可靠的合规证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 欧盟《AI法案》要求高风险系统提供方提交描述系统决策机制的技术文档，而机理可解释性（尤其是电路发现）被视为获取此类证据的最成熟途径。但作者质疑：在真实合规审核场景下，两个合格分析人员面对同一系统、同一工具，采用不同但均可辩护的设置时，所得证据是否还能保持一致、可靠。

Method: 作者预先注册了一个由七个分析轴构成的交叉网格，每个取值均取自已发表实现，并通过确定性"主张映射"将每个发现的电路转化为结构化的Annex IV合规陈述。在GPT-2 small及间接宾语识别任务上，共生成15,840项预注册规格（其中7,561项成功产出主张），评估不同规格对之间陈述的翻转率、模态主张占比，并以"可归档性基准(filability criterion)"判断证据是否通过符合性评估机构可接受的标准；还通过固定或移除评估指标、电路规模等因素进行稳健性分析，并计算电路间Jaccard重叠与Cohen's kappa。

Result: 在73.2%的规格对之间（95% CI 0.725–0.738），导出的Annex IV陈述发生翻转，模态主张仅占41.1%的空间，证据在符合性评估机构可合理接受的任何容差下均不满足可归档性标准。即使标准化评估指标（最具影响的单一选择），翻转率仍为59.4%；从主张中完全移除并固定电路规模后，翻转率降至27.1%（95% CI 0.255–0.286），仍高于预注册阈值。底层电路在结构上近乎不相交（中位成对Jaccard重叠4%），功能上无相关（Cohen's kappa 0.015），说明不稳定并非"同一机制的不同表述"所致。此外，七个已记录的发现目标中有一个在库自身的规范任务上完全无法执行。

Conclusion: 电路发现目前尚不能作为满足欧盟AI法案合规要求的可靠证据，其发现的决策机制在不同合理设置下高度不稳定。作者提出可将"可归档性基准"作为独立协议供合规审查使用，但研究仅覆盖单一模型和单一任务，该结论是否在更大规模上成立尚待验证。

Abstract: The EU AI Act requires providers of high-risk systems to file technical documentation describing how the system reaches its decisions. Mechanistic interpretability is the obvious source of such evidence, and circuit discovery is its most developed instrument. We ask whether that evidence survives the condition under which it would be relied upon: two competent analysts, the same system, the same tool, different defensible settings.
  We pre-registered a crossed grid of seven analytic axes, every level taken from a published implementation, and mapped each discovered circuit through a deterministic claim map to a structured Annex IV statement. Across 15,840 pre-registered specifications on GPT-2 small and the indirect object identification task, of which 7,561 produced a claim, the derived statement flips across 73.2% of specification pairs (95% CI 0.725 to 0.738) and the modal claim commands 41.1% of the space. The evidence fails a filability criterion at every tolerance a conformity assessment body would plausibly accept.
  Standardising the single most influential choice, the evaluation metric, leaves the flip rate at 59.4%. Removing circuit size from the claim entirely and holding it fixed leaves 27.1% (95% CI 0.255 to 0.286), still above the pre-registered threshold. The circuits underlying these claims are structurally near-disjoint, median pairwise Jaccard overlap 4%, and functionally uncorrelated at Cohen's kappa 0.015, so the instability is not one mechanism described in different words.
  We give the filability criterion as a standalone protocol, and we report that one of the seven documented discovery objectives does not execute at all on the library's own canonical task. The study covers one model and one task, and whether the conclusion holds at scale is untested.

</details>


### [222] [Simulation-Aware In-Context Policy Improvement for LLM-Aided Analog Layout Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13767)
*Bingyang Liu,Ziming Wei,Xiaohan Gao,David Z. Pan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仿真感知的LLM多智能体框架，通过“行动-观察-反思”循环在紧凑结构化布局表示上进行上下文策略改进（ICPI），仅需数十次后仿真即可超越内置启发式与贝叶斯优化调参。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模拟IC版图设计是仿真驱动的迭代过程，现有端到端版图生成器仍需专家手动调优并反复做寄生提取与后仿真；贝叶斯优化在版图层面需要数百至上千次昂贵评估而不实用；LLM虽有样本效率优势，但缺乏几何版图上下文与设计启发式，难以有效操控版图优化过程。

Method: 提出仿真感知的LLM多智能体框架，对模拟版图生成器暴露的优化参数执行上下文策略改进（ICPI），通过“行动-观察-反思”循环迭代更新参数，并利用紧凑的结构化版图表示弥补LLM对几何上下文和设计启发式的不足。

Result: 在真实模拟电路上的实验表明，仅用数十次后仿真，该方法的后仿真性能即优于版图生成器内置启发式以及基于贝叶斯优化的调参方法。

Conclusion: 该LLM多智能体ICPI框架显著提升了模拟IC版图优化的样本效率，以极少的后仿真次数获得优于传统启发式和贝叶斯优化的性能，为仿真驱动的版图设计调优提供了实用方案。

Abstract: Analog IC layout design remains a labor-intensive iterative process dominated by simulation-driven refinement. Although end-to-end layout generators accelerate initial placement and routing, they still require experts to manually tune layout optimization parameters with repeated post-layout simulations for stringent design specifications. While Bayesian Optimization (BO) is widely adopted for parameter tuning in analog IC design, at the layout level it typically requires hundreds to thousands of evaluations, each involving costly parasitic extraction and post-layout simulation, which makes it impractical. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated potential in improving the sample efficiency of such simulation-driven tuning. However, their restricted access to geometric layout context and design-specific heuristics limits their ability to manipulate the layout optimization process. In this paper, we propose a simulation-aware LLM multi-agent framework that performs in-context policy improvement (ICPI) by iteratively updating layout optimization parameters exposed by an analog layout generator through an act-observe-reflect loop on compact structured layout representations. Experiments on real-world analog circuits show that, with only tens of post-layout simulations, our approach improves post-layout performance over the generator's built-in heuristics and BO-based tuning method.

</details>


### [223] [FLARE MCMC: Fidelity-based Layer-Adaptive REcursive proposals for MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13774)
*Harini Venkatesan,Christian Shelton,Ming-Feng Ho,Simeon Bird,Mengxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FLARE MCMC 是一种利用低精度似然近似来加速采样的多层多保真 MCMC 方法，无需似然具有特定形式，可在相同计算时间内获得更大的有效样本量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCMC 在复杂模型推断中虽然只需能计算似然，但混合速度慢，需要大量样本才能得到良好估计，整体计算成本高。针对科学与工程应用中普遍存在可通过调整分辨率或精度来调节的仿真模型，希望利用低精度似然近似来改善混合并提升整体性能。

Method: 提出 FLARE MCMC，一种多层多保真 MCMC 方法，利用比真实似然计算更低精度的近似来改善混合；采用递归的分层马尔可夫链，并配以简单的层调优策略，不要求似然具有任何特定形式或内部数学结构。

Result: 在水文学和宇宙学等不同科学领域中，实验证明 FLARE MCMC 在相同计算时间内获得了更大的有效样本量（ESS），表明其整体性能优于传统方法。

Conclusion: FLARE MCMC 通过低精度似然加速混合，能够以更低的计算成本获得更好的估计，是一种通用、易调优的多保真 MCMC 方法，适用于可调精度仿真模型的科学工程推断。

Abstract: Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) requires only the ability to evaluate the likelihood, making it a common technique for inference in complex models. However, it can have a slow mixing rate, requiring the generation of many samples to obtain good estimates and an overall high computational cost. FLARE MCMC is a multi-fidelity layered MCMC method that exploits lower-fidelity approximations of the true likelihood calculation to improve mixing and leads to overall faster performance. Such lower-fidelity likelihoods are commonly available in scientific and engineering applications where the model involves a simulation whose resolution or accuracy can be tuned. Our technique uses recursive, layered chains with simple layer tuning; it does not require the likelihood to take any form or have any particular internal mathematical structure. We demonstrate experimentally that FLARE MCMC achieves larger effective sample sizes for the same computational time across different scientific domains including hydrology and cosmology.

</details>


### [224] [From Passive Delegates to Strategic Negotiators: Reinforcing Social Reasoning in Small Language Models with SocialRL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13787)
*Wenyue Hua,Zachary Huang,Tyler Payne,Safoora Yousefi,Saleema Amershi,Asli Celikyilmaz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SocialRL，一种直接训练社会推理的方法，并应用于一个 4B 模型，覆盖六个主从（principal-driven）协商与交易环境。结果显示：域内训练可达到前沿水平；跨域迁移遵循博弈结构；通过级联强化学习和多教师在线策略蒸馏可将各域专家合并为统一模型，其平均效用超过 GPT-5 系列；显式的心智理论（ToM）脚手架仅在参与训练时有效，其中仅"下一步动作预测"能预测谈判结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI 智能体日益代替用户处理谈判、比价、议价等任务，常常面对目标冲突的对手。但让助手"友好、乐于助人"的特质反而会使其成为糟糕的代理人——未经提示就泄露委托人的隐私，或在遇到阻力时轻易让步。现有方法缺乏对角色的社会推理能力的直接训练，导致模型在对抗性委托任务中表现差。

Method: 提出 SocialRL 通用配方，直接训练社会推理，并将其应用于一个 4B 模型，覆盖六个领域：Deal-or-No-Deal、CaSiNo、Craigslist、Job Interview、Calendar、Marketplace。每个领域都以同一配方进行域内训练，并在全部六个环境上评估每个策略。进一步探索跨域迁移结构，采用级联强化学习（cascade RL）与多教师在线策略蒸馏（OPD）合并专家模型；并研究显式心智理论（ToM）脚手架，包括蒸馏 ToM 轨迹与动作，以及下一步动作预测和信念预测两种 ToM 技能。

Result: 1）域内训练达到前沿：在留出场景上，4B 模型在每个领域匹配或超越 GPT-5 系列，在谈判游戏上弥合了基线到前沿差距的 73-122%，78% 的买家开盘锚定在目标价以下，而未训练模型仅 3%；2）跨域迁移遵循博弈结构：结构配对的游戏相互提升，一个广泛的多议题"捐赠者"领域几乎提升所有领域，而结构孤立的游戏完全不迁移；3）依据迁移结构，级联 RL 与多教师 OPD 将各领域专家合并为统一 4B 模型，在六个环境取得 0.627 的平均效用，匹配或超过 GPT-4.1（0.625）、GPT-5.1（0.619）、GPT-5.2（0.613）；4）显式 ToM 脚手架仅在参与训练时有效：蒸馏 ToM 轨迹而非仅动作，在所有环境上提升效用且泛化更好，两种 ToM 技能中只有下一步动作预测能预测谈判结果。

Conclusion: SocialRL 证明，通过直接训练社会推理，一个小型 4B 模型即可在多个主从协商领域匹配甚至超越大型前沿模型。跨域迁移严格遵循博弈结构，且该结构可指导将专家策略合并为统一模型。显式 ToM 信号只有在被纳入训练时才带来收益，其中下一步动作预测是关键技能。这表明社会推理是可训练、可迁移、可合并的能力，为构建胜任委托任务的智能体提供了通用路径。

Abstract: AI agents increasingly act on their users' behalf, handling tasks such as scheduling meetings, comparing offers, and haggling over prices. These principal-driven tasks routinely place the agent across from a counterpart (another user's agent, a seller, a recruiter) whose goals may conflict with its principal's. Yet the dispositions that make an assistant pleasant can make it a poor delegate: a friendly, helpful frontier model may disclose its principal's private information unprompted and concede at the first sign of resistance. We present SocialRL, a general recipe that trains social reasoning directly, and apply it to a 4B model across six domains: Deal-or-No-Deal, CaSiNo, Craigslist, Job Interview, Calendar, and Marketplace. Every domain is trained in-domain under the same recipe, and every policy is evaluated on all six. We find that (1) in-domain training reaches the frontier: on held-out scenarios the 4B matches or exceeds the GPT-5 family per domain, closing 73-122% of the baseline-to-frontier gap on the negotiation games, with 78% of buyer openings anchoring below target versus 3% untrained; (2) cross-domain transfer follows game structure: structurally paired games lift each other, a broad multi-issue donor lifts nearly all domains, and structurally isolated games transfer nothing; (3) guided by this transfer structure, two strategies, cascade RL and multi-teacher on-policy distillation (OPD), consolidate the per-domain specialists into a single unified 4B that reaches 0.627 average utility across all six environments, matching or exceeding GPT-4.1 (0.625), GPT-5.1 (0.619), and GPT-5.2 (0.613); (4) an explicit theory-of-mind scaffold helps only through training: distilling the ToM trace, rather than actions alone, lifts utility on every environment and generalizes better across them, and of the two ToM skills, only next-action prediction predicts negotiation outcomes.

</details>


### [225] [SDO: Subspace Deconflicting Operator for Multi-Adapter Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13820)
*Zhongsheng Wang,Zhedong Lin,Qian Liu,Xinyu Zhang,Jiamou Liu*

Main category: cs.AI
